Result: 在EvTTC上取得9.59%相对TTC误差，端到端推理延迟4.6 ms，与最佳学习方法相差0.15个百分点且无需目标先验。该延迟对应约4 cm车辆运动，而最快竞争方法为1 m。支持随时TTC预测、零样本迁移与INT8量化，每32768个事件的能耗估计由18.5降至2.8 mJ。

Conclusion: 事件驱动的脉冲状态空间动力学可为反应式机器人系统提供低延迟、持续更新的时间感知能力。

Abstract: Robotic systems operating in dynamic environments require visual perception that evolves continuously with the incoming sensory stream. Event cameras provide microsecond temporal resolution and asynchronous sensing, but most learning-based methods accumulate events into frames or temporal bins, introducing an integration delay that can limit fast reaction. Here we propose REACT, a fully spiking state-space model for event-driven temporal perception that processes raw events one by one, without temporal accumulation. REACT uses a complex-valued spiking neuron, C-SiLIF, whose continuous-time dynamics are driven by the physical inter-event interval, allowing its internal state to evolve at the temporal resolution of individual events. We evaluate REACT on gesture recognition and time-to-collision (TTC) estimation from full-field event streams, without a target bounding box or localization input. On EvTTC, REACT achieves a 9.59% relative TTC error with 4.6 ms end-to-end inference latency, within 0.15 percentage points of the best learned method while requiring no target prior. At the dataset's mean approach speed, this latency corresponds to only 4 cm of vehicle motion, compared with 1 m for the fastest competing learned method. REACT further supports anytime TTC prediction, zero-shot transfer to a different driving sequence, and INT8 quantization, reducing the estimated energy consumption from 18.5 to 2.8 mJ per 32,768 events. These results show that event-driven spiking state-space dynamics can provide low-latency, continuously updated temporal perception for reactive robotic systems.

</details>


### [106] [4D Radar Perception Algorithms for Autonomous Driving: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19216)
*Xumin Wu,Jun Zhou,Jilin Mei,Chen Min,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于4D毫米波雷达感知算法的综述，按感知任务与算法演进组织，涵盖从信号处理、目标检测到语义分割、运动估计、占据预测和动态场景重建的研究，并比较了纯雷达学习、多模态融合与跨模态监督/知识蒸馏，强调高度、多普勒测量和雷达物理先验的利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D毫米波雷达感知研究近年来快速发展，领域方向不断扩展，但研究脉络分散，缺乏以任务为导向的系统梳理，需要一篇综述整合各任务方向的算法、数据集与评估体系。

Method: 综述方法：先介绍雷达基础、数据表示与质量增强方法；随后按目标级感知、运动与定位、局部与稠密空间感知、动态场景理解逐层回顾；在每个方向比较纯雷达学习、多模态融合、跨模态监督与知识蒸馏；重点分析高度、多普勒测量与雷达物理先验在各类任务中的应用；并汇总现有数据集的覆盖任务、输入数据、标注与评估协议。

Result: 给出了各感知任务方向的研究现状对比；总结了高度、多普勒与物理先验的跨任务利用方式；梳理了现有数据集的覆盖任务、输入数据、标注与评测标准，明确了不同研究方向的经验支撑情况。

Conclusion: 综述以任务为导向，呈现了4D雷达感知从稀疏目标感知向动态空间理解的转变；指出该领域的共性挑战与面向自动驾驶的未来方向，为后续研究提供统一框架与参考。

Abstract: Research on 4D millimeter-wave radar perception algorithms has flourished in recent years, extending from signal processing and object detection to semantic segmentation, motion estimation, occupancy prediction, and dynamic scene reconstruction. This review organizes the field according to the evolution of perception tasks and algorithms. It first introduces radar fundamentals, data representations, and quality-enhancement methods, and then reviews object-level perception, motion and localization, local and dense spatial perception, and dynamic scene understanding. Across these directions, we compare radar-only learning, multimodal fusion, and cross-modal supervision and knowledge distillation. Particular attention is paid to how elevation, Doppler measurements, and radar physical priors are exploited across tasks. We further summarize the task coverage, input data, annotations, and evaluation protocols of existing datasets, clarifying the empirical support for different research directions. Finally, we discuss the common challenges and future directions of 4D radar perception for autonomous driving. This review provides a task-oriented perspective on the transition from sparse object perception to dynamic spatial understanding.

</details>


### [107] [Grasping by interconnection: robust closing motions from coarse object templates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19228)
*Julien Vanderheyden,Guillaume Drion,Fulvio Forni,Pierre Sacré*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于四个原则（粗糙物体模板、人类抓取类型、以物体为中心的交互、柔性滑动接触而非预设接触点）的抓取运动规划器，通过虚拟模型控制实现，并在Shadow灵巧手上评估。无反馈时闭合运动可容忍约1cm尺寸误差和数厘米、数十度的位姿误差，在27种条件中的25种优于最先进的数据驱动规划器，并成功抓取80个日常物体中的82.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手抓取物体时，物体的形状、尺寸和位姿往往只有近似已知。现有抓取规划器通常需要精确物体模型，或依赖反馈来纠正误差，但闭合运动本身能容忍多少不准确性尚不清楚。因此作者希望探究能否将鲁棒性直接设计进闭合运动本身，而非仅依赖反馈。

Method: 设计了基于四个原则的运动规划器：(1) 物体的粗糙模板；(2) 人类抓取类型；(3) 以物体为中心的交互；(4) 采用柔性滑动接触而非预设接触点。通过虚拟模型控制实现该规划器，并在Shadow Dexterous Hand上进行评估。

Result: 在无反馈条件下，规划的闭合运动可容忍约1cm的尺寸误差以及数厘米和数十度的位姿误差，容忍范围比最先进的数据驱动规划器更宽（27种测试条件中有25种更优）。在80个日常物体上成功抓取82.5%，并能在自主流程中完成任务。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性可以被设计进闭合运动本身，而不必仅依赖反馈。该规划器为不确定性环境下的可靠操作开辟了新路径，未来将结合物理手上的自适应反馈控制进一步推进。

Abstract: Dexterous robot hands must often grasp objects whose shape, size, and pose are known only approximately. Grasp planners typically require accurate object models or correct errors with feedback, but how much inaccuracy a closing motion can tolerate on its own remains unclear. To address this question, we designed a motion planner based on four principles: a coarse template of the object, human grasp types, an object-centric interaction, and compliant, sliding contacts instead of prescribed contact points. This paper presents the planner, implemented through virtual model control, and its evaluation on a Shadow Dexterous Hand. Without feedback, the planned closing motions tolerated size errors of about 1cm and pose errors of several centimeters and tens of degrees, a wider range than a state-of-the-art data-driven planner in 25 of 27 tested conditions. They also grasped 82.5% of 80 everyday objects and succeeded within an autonomous pipeline. Robustness can thus be designed into the closing motion itself, rather than left only to feedback. This planner opens a path toward reliable manipulation in uncertain settings, which we will pursue by combining it with adaptive feedback control on the physical hand.

</details>


### [108] [Learning Safe Humanoid Navigation from Reduced Order Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19272)
*William D. Compton,Zachary Olkin,Ryan Bena,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段方法RoM-Nav，实现人形机器人无地图多楼层导航，垂直位移超10m、路径超100m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单阶段强化学习导航流水线难以扩展到多层、多楼层地形，瓶颈在于人形机器人与复杂地形（如楼梯）交互的难度。

Method: 将导航问题分解：首先训练一个基于降阶动力学但使用完整3D LiDAR观测的策略来导航复杂多楼层地形；再用其引导一个基于全阶人形动力学的策略，并在回路中冻结行走策略；同时对导航策略输出应用泊松安全滤波器以保证分布外障碍物下的安全性。

Result: 在Unitree G1上验证，实现无地图多楼层导航，测试中垂直位移超10m、路径长度超100m；泊松安全滤波器在不降低导航成功率的前提下恢复了分布外障碍物场景的安全性。

Conclusion: 分阶段训练与降阶到全阶的知识引导，结合安全滤波器，能有效扩展人形机器人导航至多楼层复杂地形。

Abstract: Research in humanoid robotics has achieved rapid progress in locomotion, and recent results have pushed the boundary on autonomous navigation. We demonstrate that a standard single-stage RL navigation pipeline struggles to scale to multi-level and multi-story terrain, limited by the difficulty of complex humanoid terrain interactions such as stairs. To overcome this challenge, we decompose the navigation problem into two pieces. First, we train a policy operating on the reduced order dynamics but with full 3D LiDAR observations to navigate complex, multi-story terrain. We then utilize this navigation knowledge to kickstart a policy operating on the full-order humanoid dynamics, with a frozen locomotion policy in the loop. Additionally, we demonstrate that applying a Poisson safety filter to the navigation policy output recovers safety in the presence of out-of-distribution obstacles, without dropping navigation success rate. We demonstrate the resulting RoM-Nav policy on a Unitree G1, accomplishing mapless multi-floor navigation covering trials with over 10m of vertical displacement and over 100m of path length. Project page with videos https://wdc3iii.github.io/rom-nav/ .

</details>


### [109] [OHRID-Retail: An Open Multimodal Dataset of Human Activity in Retail Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19302)
*Xiangrui Wang,Yuetong Wu,Jalen Beeman,Robert Cook,Yu Gu,Nathanial Pearson,Trevor Smith,Read Hayes,Boyi Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OHRID Retail 是一个面向零售场景的开放式人体多模态数据集，采集16名健康成年人分别在无机器人、机器人低速引导和机器人高速引导三种条件下进行模拟货架拣选任务时全身运动学（17个Xsens惯性传感器）和肌肉活动（10处表面肌电）数据，每条件两次试验，并提供原始记录、处理后指标、文档与可复现分析资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 描述人类在与人共享环境的移动机器人场景中行为的数据集仍然有限，尤其缺乏同时涵盖移动、伸手、物体操作以及机器人引导下运动的零售活动数据。该缺口限制了人机共享零售等环境中的人体行为与交互研究。

Method: 采用被试内设计，16名健康成年人完成模拟货架拣选任务，设置三种条件（无机器人、机器人低速引导、机器人高速引导），每种条件完成两次试验。使用17个Xsens Awinda惯性传感器采集全身运动学，使用Delsys Trigno表面肌电传感器在10个位置采集肌肉活动。

Result: 描述性分析显示，不同机器人交互条件和不同身体部位之间的全身运动强度和肌肉激活存在差异。数据集公开提供原始记录、处理后的测量指标、文档以及可复现的分析资源。

Conclusion: OHRID Retail 可支持人类活动识别、多模态传感器融合、职业生物力学、人体工学、人感知机器人导航以及零售及相关共享环境中的人机交互等研究。

Abstract: Open datasets describing human behavior in environments shared with mobile robots remain limited, particularly for retail activities that combine locomotion, reaching, object handling, and robot guided movement. This paper introduces OHRID Retail, an open, human centered multimodal dataset collected from 16 healthy adults performing a simulated shelf picking task under three within participant conditions: no robot, low speed robot guidance, and high speed robot guidance. Each participant completed two trials per condition. Whole body kinematics were recorded using 17 Xsens Awinda inertial sensors and muscle activity was measured at 10 locations using Delsys Trigno surface electromyography sensors. Descriptive analyses demonstrate variation in whole body movement intensity and muscle activation across robot interaction conditions and body locations. OHRID Retail provides openly available raw recordings, processed measures, documentation, and reproducible analysis resources. The dataset can support research in human activity recognition, multimodal sensor fusion, occupational biomechanics, ergonomics, human aware robot navigation, and human robot interaction in retail and related shared environments.

</details>


### [110] [GAVEL: Graph World Models for Verified and Efficient Long-Horizon LLM Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19315)
*Ruiyang Wang,Hao-Lun Hsu,Swarajh Mehta,Jiwoo Kim,Zhihao Dou,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAVEL 是一个基于显式图世界模型的框架，用于验证和修复长时程 LLM 规划，在 BEHAVIOR-1K 上大幅提升单任务与多任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 虽提供灵活的长时程机器人规划接口，但生成的计划常无法满足具身约束、难以从规划错误中恢复，且在部分可观测条件下推理效果不佳，因此需要一种能验证与修复规划的方法。

Method: GAVEL 构建显式图世界模型，表示物体关系、动作前置条件与效果，以及对未观测物体位置的概率信念。该模型可在执行前预测 LLM 动作后果、检测违规，并修复可直接由世界模型推导的修正，仅将需要语义推理的错误交由 LLM 重新规划；对多任务指令，则基于物体位置分布重排剩余子任务以最小化期望搜索成本。

Result: 在 BEHAVIOR-1K 的 100 个单长时程任务和 500 条多任务指令上评测。使用 Qwen3-8B 时，单任务成功率从 41.2% 提升至 91.8%，多任务成功率从 19.9% 提升至 92.6%；分布信念推理相比静态变体减少约 5.4% 的移动距离。

Conclusion: 显式图世界模型支架能够显著提升长时程具身规划的可靠性与效率，适用于紧凑型和前沿托管式 LLM。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide a flexible interface for long-horizon robot planning, but generated plans often fail to respect embodiment constraints, recover from planning errors, or reason effectively under partial observability. We present GAVEL, a framework for verifying and repairing long-horizon LLM planning built around an explicit graph world model. The graph represents relevant object-relations, action pre-conditions and effects, and probabilistic beliefs over unobserved object locations. This model can predict the consequences of LLM-generated actions before execution, detect violations, and repair those whose corrections follow directly from the world model. This method also reserves LLM replanning solely for errors requiring semantic reasoning. For multi-task instructions, GAVEL reasons over distributions of possible object locations to reorder remaining subtasks and minimize expected search cost. We evaluate GAVEL on BEHAVIOR-1K across 100 single long-horizon tasks and 500 multi-task instructions. With Qwen3-8B, GAVEL improves single-task success from 41.2% to 91.8% and multi-task success from 19.9% to 92.6%. Distributional belief reasoning also reduces travel distance by approximately 5.4% compared with a static variant. These improvements show that an explicit graph world model harness can substantially improve the reliability and efficiency of long-horizon embodied planning across compact and frontier hosted LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [111] [A Morphing Aerial Robot With Thruster-Integrated Flexible Continuum Links for Shape Adaptive Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19328)
*Eri Sawada,Kazuki Sugihara,Ayano Miyamichi,Kunio Kojima,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带柔性连续体连杆的变形旋翼分布式空中机器人，同时实现高形状适应性与扩大的力旋量空间，首次实现集成推进器的柔性连杆空中操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中操作是拓展空中机器人应用的关键。实现通用空中操作的两大方向为：(i) 利用软体机械臂获得高环境适应性；(ii) 将推进器沿机械臂分布以扩大可行力旋量空间。然而现有空中机器人尚未同时满足这两项要求。

Method: 提出带柔性连续体连杆的变形旋翼分布式空中机器人：柔性连续体连杆作为软体机械臂，被动顺应环境形状；沿连杆分布的推进器扩大可行推力旋量空间，使末端执行器能施加较大交互力。为抑制轻质连续体连杆的振动，开发了兼具高扭转与垂直刚度的复合板簧结构及抑振控制方法。

Result: 通过上述实现，展示了稳定飞行及多种空中操作任务。据作者所知，这是首个使用集成推进器的柔性连杆实现空中操作的工作。

Conclusion: 该研究首次将柔性连续体连杆与分布式推进器结合，同时实现形状适应性与扩大的力旋量空间，并验证了稳定飞行与多种操作任务，为通用空中操作提供了新方案。

Abstract: In recent years, aerial manipulation has attracted increasing attention as a key to expand the application of aerial robots. In this work, we focus on two major research directions for achieving versatile aerial manipulation: (i) acquiring high environmental adaptability using soft manipulators, and (ii) expanding the feasible wrench space by distributing thrusters along the manipulator. However, no aerial robot has simultaneously satisfied these two requirements. Therefore, in this paper, we propose a morphing rotor-distributed aerial robot with flexible continuum links that achieves both high shape adaptability and an expanded wrench space. The flexible continuum links function as soft manipulators, passively conforming to the shape of the environment, while the thrusters distributed along the continuum links expand the feasible thrust wrench space and enable the end-effector to exert large interaction forces. To realize the proposed robot, it is essential to suppress vibrations of the lightweight continuum links. Thus, we develop a composite leaf-spring structure that provides both high torsional and vertical stiffness, and vibration-suppressing control methods. Using these implementations, we demonstrate stable flight and a variety of aerial manipulation tasks. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to realize aerial manipulations using flexible links with an integrated thruster.

</details>


### [112] [SemSafe-3DGS: Semantic Risk-Aware Active Navigation in Uncertain 3D Gaussian Splatting Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19330)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Athanasios Cosse,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种语义风险感知的安全主动感知导航框架，在带属性的3D高斯地图中利用类别依赖风险权重调制AVaR碰撞间隙模型，并与主动感知屏障统一集成到CBF-QP中，实现语义相关的安全约束与轨迹自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境中的自主机器人需在安全导航的同时获取有助于未来规划的观测。现有安全方法主要基于几何进行推理，导致几何相似但语义后果不同的场景元素产生相近的控制响应，忽略了语义差异对安全的影响。

Method: 在带属性的3D高斯地图中，用类别依赖的风险权重调制平均风险价值（AVaR）碰撞间隙模型，使安全关键的高斯基元在复合障碍函数中占更大权重；将加权间隙聚合为控制屏障函数（CBF），并设计轨迹相关的主动感知屏障以降低机器人预期运动路径上的几何地图不确定性；两者统一集成在CBF-QP中，将语义风险感知避碰作为硬约束，在信息获取与安全或任务进度冲突时放松信息项。

Result: 实验表明该方法能高效施加安全约束、通过主动感知改善导航、实现语义相关的轨迹自适应，并在Ackermann动力学真机上完成执行。

Conclusion: 通过将语义风险引入安全屏障并与主动感知统一优化，该方法弥补了几何式安全建模的不足，提升了部分可观测环境下导航的安全性与信息获取效率，并具备实机可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in partially observed environments must navigate safely while acquiring observations that improve future planning. Existing safety formulations generally reason primarily about geometry. Consequently, geometrically similar scene elements may induce comparable control responses despite having different semantic consequences. We present a semantic risk aware safe-active perception framework for navigation in attributed 3D Gaussian maps. Semantic attributes modulate an Average Value-at-Risk collision clearance model through class dependent risk weights, allowing safety-critical Gaussian primitives to receive greater influence in the composite barrier. The resulting weighted clearances are aggregated into a control barrier function, while a trajectory-relevant active perception barrier promotes observations that reduce geometric map uncertainty along the robot's anticipated motion. Both objectives are integrated in a unified CBF-QP that enforces semantic risk-aware collision avoidance as a hard constraint while relaxing information acquisition when it conflicts with safety or task progress. Experiments demonstrate efficient safety constraint, improved navigation through active perception, semantic dependent trajectory adaptation, and real-robot execution under Ackermann dynamics.

</details>


### [113] [Dynamic-LIVO: A Dynamic-Aware LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry System Using Spatio-Temporal Normals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19336)
*Zhixin Zhang,Samuel Ahiwe,Matthew Hale,Liang Zhao,Pawel Ladosz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Dynamic-LIVO，一个面向动态环境的动态感知LiDAR-惯性-视觉里程计系统，通过时空法线分析与时间延迟估计策略实现鲁棒的状态估计与静态彩色建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态环境中，动态物体会对LiDAR-惯性-视觉里程计的状态估计与建图造成干扰，导致定位精度下降和地图污染，因此需要一种能够识别并剔除动态观测的鲁棒系统。

Method: 采用时空（S-T）法线分析识别动态LiDAR点，并将分类结果传播至LiDAR-惯性与视觉-惯性更新，防止动态观测影响估计与建图；针对新观测和稀疏区域S-T法线估计不可靠的问题，引入时间延迟S-T法线估计策略，推迟对约束不足点的分类并在获得更多观测后重新评估。

Result: 在公开数据集和自采集数据集（多种传感器配置）上的大量实验表明，Dynamic-LIVO在复杂动态环境中提升了定位精度，并生成更干净的静态彩色地图。

Conclusion: Dynamic-LIVO能够有效应对动态环境，兼顾动态分类可靠性与静态点保留，实现鲁棒状态估计与静态彩色建图，源代码与自采集数据集将在录用后公开。

Abstract: This paper proposes Dynamic-LIVO, a dynamic-aware LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry (LIVO) system for robust state estimation and static colored mapping in dynamic environments. Dynamic-LIVO employs Spatio-Temporal (S-T) normal analysis to identify dynamic LiDAR points and propagates the resulting classification to both LiDAR-inertial and visual-inertial updates, preventing dynamic LiDAR measurements and their associated visual observations from affecting state estimation and mapping. However, S-T normal estimation can be unreliable in newly observed and spatially sparse regions due to insufficient spatio-temporal observations. To address this issue, we introduce a time-delayed S-T normal estimation strategy that defers the classification of insufficiently constrained points and re-evaluates them as additional observations become available. This strategy improves dynamic classification reliability while preserving valid static points for map construction. Extensive experiments on public and self-collected datasets with diverse sensor configurations demonstrate that Dynamic-LIVO improves localization accuracy and produces cleaner static colored maps in challenging dynamic environments. The source code and self-collected dataset will be publicly released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [114] [ViLoMan: Learning Visual-Proprioceptive Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation Skills for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19340)
*Zejie Tian,Ruibing Hou,Bingpeng Ma,Börje F. Karlsson,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViLoMan将部分人类-物体交互运动学示范补全为物理可执行的机器人轨迹，再通过教师-学生蒸馏学习统一全身策略，仅用本体感知与自我中心深度即可完成人形机器人自主移动-操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人移动-操作需要全身自适应协调，但缺乏多样且物理可执行的机器人-物体交互数据，且难以直接从机载观测学习统一的全身控制策略，因此自主移动-操作仍具挑战。

Method: 框架分两步：先将人类-物体交互的部分运动学示范转化为完整、物理可执行的机器人轨迹；再在这些轨迹上采用教师-学生蒸馏框架，学习将自我中心深度观测与本体感知测量直接映射到关节级全身动作的统一策略。部署时无需参考动作或中间指令。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界中，针对多种门配置与机器人初始条件评估关门任务。单一策略使Unitree G1仅依靠机载深度传感器与本体感知完成完整任务，并在任务变化下稳健泛化，实现有效的仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: ViLoMan提供了一种可扩展的自主人形移动-操作框架，验证了从部分人类示范到统一全身策略的可行性与泛化能力，项目页面：viloman-anonymous.pages.dev。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires adaptive whole-body coordination to seamlessly integrate locomotion and physical interaction. Despite recent advances, learning autonomous loco-manipulation remains challenging due to the scarcity of diverse, physically executable robot-object interaction data and the difficulty of learning unified whole-body control directly from onboard observations. We present ViLoMan, a scalable framework for autonomous humanoid loco-manipulation. ViLoMan first transforms partial kinematic demonstrations of human-object interactions into complete, physically executable robot trajectories. It then leverages these trajectories within a teacher-student distillation framework to learn a unified policy that maps egocentric depth observations and proprioceptive measurements directly to joint-level whole-body actions. During deployment, the policy requires neither reference motions nor intermediate commands. We evaluate ViLoMan on door-closing tasks across diverse door configurations and robot initial conditions in both simulation and the real world. Experimental results demonstrate that a single policy enables a Unitree G1 humanoid to complete the full task using only onboard depth sensing and proprioception, while generalizing robustly across task variations and transferring effectively from simulation to reality. Project page: viloman-anonymous.pages.dev.

</details>


### [115] [Kinematics-Grounded Agentic AI for Robotic Additive Manufacturing Process Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19347)
*Jingzhan Ge,Ruimin Chen,Azadeh Haghighi,Jiong Tang,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 A-RAM（agentic robotic additive manufacturing）框架，将用户意图与零件文件转化为可执行、可追溯的机器人增材制造规划，通过 LLM 解读目标并生成约束请求、确定性 Planning Agent 执行搜索、领域工具提供切片/放置/逆运动学等量化证据，并在六轴机械臂 AM 单元上用三类案例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人增材制造使路径规划依赖具体机器人，切片软件生成的路径在零件坐标系下看似优越，但在机械臂上可能不可行或动力学不友好；零件朝向与工作空间布局共同影响运动学实现。现有 AM 工具、基于 LLM 的决策支持与数字孪生系统均无法对切片决策与朝向/放置这类耦合决策进行集成的事前评估。

Method: 提出 A-RAM，一种 agent-specialist-tool 框架：LLM 负责解读制造目标与约束、识别既定变量与可搜索规划变量，并以 schema 约束的请求编码推理；确定性 Planning Agent 据此实例化相应搜索工作流；领域工具计算切片、放置、逆运动学、轨迹时序、Joint-6 jerk 与挤出等量化证据。

Result: 在六轴机械臂 AM 单元上开展三类案例研究，涵盖专家指定规划、仅目标规划、目标相关填充筛选、几何相关朝向-放置选择。所选方案相比最不利的有效候选，最大 Joint-6 jerk 降低最多 53.5%，平均绝对 Joint-6 jerk 降低最多 48.3%；目标特定填充筛选使运动规划完成时间缩短最多 40.1%，挤出路径缩短最多 12.7%。

Conclusion: A-RAM 能够将用户意图与零件文件转化为可追溯、可执行的机器人增材制造方案，并在切片决策与机器人运动学实现之间进行集成的事前评估，验证了其在降低 jerk、缩短规划时间与挤出路径方面的优势。

Abstract: Robotic additive manufacturing (AM) extends material-extrusion printing beyond gantry kinematics but makes process planning robot-dependent. A slicer-generated plan that appears favorable in part coordinates can become infeasible or robotically unfavorable on a manipulator because slicer-process decisions and part orientation determine the generated path, while part orientation and workspace placement affect its kinematic realization. Existing AM tools, large language model (LLM)-based decision-support methods, and digital-shadow systems do not provide integrated pre-execution evaluation of these coupled decisions. This paper presents agentic robotic additive manufacturing (A-RAM), an agent-specialist-tool framework that converts user intent and a part file into traceable, execution-ready plans. The LLM interprets manufacturing objectives and constraints, identifies prescribed and searchable planning variables, and encodes this reasoning in a schema-constrained request; a deterministic Planning Agent instantiates the corresponding search workflow, while domain tools compute quantitative evidence for slicing, placement, inverse kinematics, trajectory timing, Joint-6 jerk, and extrusion. The framework is evaluated on a six-axis robotic-arm AM cell through three case studies covering expert-specified planning, goal-only planning, objective-dependent infill screening, and geometry-dependent orientation-placement selection. Across the evaluated candidate sets, selected plans achieve up to 53.5% lower maximum Joint-6 jerk and 48.3% lower mean absolute Joint-6 jerk than the least favorable valid candidates, while objective-specific infill screening yields motion-plan completion times up to 40.1% shorter and extrusion paths up to 12.7% shorter than the corresponding least favorable screened patterns.

</details>


### [116] [Design and Experimental Validation of a 3D Printed Torsional Series Elastic Actuator for Safe Human Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19367)
*Joel Hidalgo Pisco,Melissa Cobos Condo,Luigi Miranda,Dennys Paillacho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并验证了一种用于串联弹性驱动器（SEA）的3D打印TPU低刚度扭转弹簧，实现高线性扭矩-变形响应，并配合混合位置控制器实现安全的人机物理交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会与服务机器人需保证人机物理交互的本征安全。传统金属SEA虽提供硬件柔顺性，但往往需要复杂的多部件装配，成本高、制造难，因此需要一种低成本、易制造且安全的柔顺元件与配套控制方案。

Method: 采用3D打印热塑性聚氨酯（TPU）制造低刚度扭转弹簧，进行有限元分析（FEA）与实验验证；针对标准位置受限伺服电机，设计带扭矩阈值切换的混合位置控制器，并开展非稳态轨迹跟踪与外力屈服实验。

Result: 柔顺元件呈现高度线性的扭矩-变形响应（Ks = 0.066 Nm/度），与数值预测偏差小于3%（差异归因于FDM结构各向异性）；系统能准确跟踪非稳态轨迹并安全屈服于外部扰动；TPU的固有材料阻尼起到被动低通滤波作用，抑制控制模式切换时的高频振荡。

Conclusion: 该架构为安全的人机物理交互提供了一种低成本、可靠且易于制造的解决方案。

Abstract: Ensuring intrinsic safety in physical human robot interaction (pHRI) is a critical requirement for social and service robots. While Series Elastic Actuators (SEAs) offer hardware based compliance, traditional metallic designs often require complex, multi part assemblies. This paper presents the design, finite element analysis (FEA), and experimental validation of a low stiffness, torsional spring for SEAs, manufactured via 3D printed thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU). The compliant element exhibits a highly linear torque deformation response (Ks = 0.066 Nm/degree), matching numerical predictions with under 3% deviation, a variance attributed to FDM structural anisotropy. To accommodate external interactions using standard position limited servomotors, a hybrid position controller with torque threshold switching was implemented. Experimental evaluations demonstrate the system ability to accurately track non stationary trajectories and safely yield to external disturbances. Furthermore, the inherent material damping of the TPU acts as a passive low pass filter, preventing high frequency oscillations during control mode transitions. The proposed architecture offers a cost effective, reliable, and easily manufacturable solution for safe pHRI.

</details>


### [117] [Enhancing the Perception of Safety and Comfort during Physical Human-Robot Handshake Interactions by Integrating Flexible Elements into a Robotic Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19375)
*Joel Hidalgo,Dennys Paillacho,Melissa Cobos,Luigi Miranda*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过实验研究，比较刚性关节与串联弹性驱动器（SEA）关节的机械臂在握手交互中的差异，发现SEA能提升用户感知的安全性与舒适度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机物理交互中的安全与舒适是社交机器人发展的关键问题，而握手这类直接身体接触的自然手势尤为具有挑战性。作者希望验证串联弹性驱动器（SEA）吸收冲击的设计策略是否能改善交互体验，并明确刚性关节与弹性关节的差异，从而提升机器人系统在日常场景中的物理安全和社会接受度。

Method: 招募10名志愿者（6男4女），每人分别与两种机械臂握手各一次：一种为刚性关节，另一种为采用SEA的柔性关节。测试中采集末端执行器的客观轨迹数据，并通过感知问卷收集关于安全性、自然性和信任感的主观评价。

Result: 问卷结果显示，装配SEA的机械臂在安全性和舒适度方面获得更高的主观评分，表明弹性元件有助于改善用户对交互的感知。

Conclusion: SEA在机械臂中的引入有利于提升人机握手交互中的感知安全与舒适，支持其作为促进更安全、更易被社会接受的人机交互的设计方案。

Abstract: Safety and comfort in human-robot physical in-teractions are essential aspects in the development of social technologies, where natural gestures, such as handshakes, represent a challenge due to their direct physical contact. The implementation of series elastic actuators (SEA) to absorb impacts is proposed as a design strategy that favors safer interactions. This paper presents an experimental study aimed at evaluating how the incorporation of SEAs in robotic arms influences perceived safety and the interaction experience dur-ing handshaking. The design allows a direct comparison of the effect of rigidity versus the incorporation of elastic elements, in order to identify the advantages of SEAs in improving the physical safety and social acceptance of robotic systems in everyday contexts. The experiment was carried out with 10 volunteers (6 men and 4 women), who performed two interactions with each robotic arm: one with rigid joints and the other with flexible joints using SEA. During testing, objective data on end-effector trajectories were collected, as well as subjective information through a perception survey focused on safety, naturalness, and confidence during the handshake. The survey results show increased perceptions of safety and comfort with the SEA-equipped arm, supporting its potential to facilitate safer and more socially accepted human-robot interactions.

</details>


### [118] [Task-Oriented Active Learning of Residual Dynamics for Model Predictive Path Integral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19378)
*Nobuaki Aoki,Hojin Lee,Stefan Sosnowski,Sandra Hirche*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ToIA主动学习准则，用于MPPI控制中的在线GP残差学习，优先采集能降低后续状态预测不确定性且与任务相关的观测，显著提升越野导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线残差学习可减小预测控制中的模型失配，但被动数据采集难以覆盖任务后期才重要的状态；而任务无关的主动学习虽关注不确定或信息量大的区域，这些区域获得的信息未必能改善任务表现。

Method: ToIA针对MPPI与在线GP残差学习：对每个采样的控制序列，估计rollout早期观测能在多大程度上降低同一rollout后续状态的预测不确定性，并用该rollout与任务的相关性加权；该评分直接在现有MPPI rollout批次上评估，无需采样未来观测或基于假设后验更新重新优化控制。

Result: 在异构地形的留出地图越野导航仿真中，ToIA相比被动GP学习将到达目标成功率提高19.3和27.4个百分点，并在密集与稀疏在线学习间隔下均优于任务无关主动学习基线；消融表明稀疏模型更新时任务相关性尤为重要；实现支持在NVIDIA RTX 2080 Ti上以20 Hz进行在线控制。

Conclusion: 面向任务的主动信息获取能有效提升在线残差学习预测控制的性能，且计算开销低、可实时运行。

Abstract: Online residual learning can reduce model mismatch in predictive control, but passive data collection may fail to adequately cover states that become important later in the task. Task-agnostic active learning targets uncertain or informative regions, but information acquired in such regions does not necessarily improve task performance. This paper introduces Task-Oriented Information Acquisition (ToIA), an active-learning criterion for model predictive path integral control (MPPI) with online Gaussian process (GP) residual learning. For each sampled control sequence, ToIA estimates how much an observation obtained early in the rollout would reduce predictive uncertainty at later states on the same rollout, and weights this reduction by the rollout's relevance to the task. The score is evaluated over the existing MPPI rollout batch without sampling future observations or re-optimizing control under hypothetical posterior updates. In simulated off-road navigation across held-out maps with heterogeneous terrain, ToIA improved the goal-reaching success rate over passive GP learning by 19.3 and 27.4 percentage points and outperformed task-agnostic active-learning baselines across dense and sparse online-learning intervals. An ablation study indicates that task relevance is particularly important under sparse model updates. The implementation supports online control at 20 Hz on an NVIDIA RTX 2080 Ti.

</details>


### [119] [From Rollout to Reset: A Graph-Based Harness for Autonomous Long-Horizon Manipulation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19413)
*Jing Jiang,Yue Yang,Xinkai Jiang,Gedas Bertasius,Daniel J. Szafir,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALTER：基于场景图与LLM规划的自主长时程任务评估与场景重置系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人评估需人工重置场景，耗时且初始状态分布不明确，复现性差；已有AutoEval仅适用于单步任务，长时程任务终止状态组合繁多，单一重置策略无法覆盖。

Method: HALTER通过在线构建空间场景图（点云＋视觉基础模型），由LLM基于场景图评分rollout、规划重置并验证重置成功；重置通过在学习到的原子重置技能库上规划实现，示范成本随技能库规模而非终止状态数增长，且无需任何任务的标注成功图像。

Result: 在Franka机械臂四项长时程任务上：重置成功率76%（AutoEval 52%、运动规划重置65%）；完成技能比例估计正确率90%（对比76%）；重置验证判定正确率91%（AutoEval 78%）；评估流程操作员时间减少72%。三项留出任务组合泛化重置率74.7%（逐任务策略1.3%），并消融了场景表示与图更新频率。

Conclusion: HALTER以技能库规划＋场景图推理实现长时程任务的自动重置与评估验证，显著优于单步基线，具备组合泛化能力并大幅降低人工成本。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies are improving quickly, and real-robot evaluation remains the standard evidence for that progress. It still relies on a human to reset the scene between rollouts, which consumes operator time and leaves the initial state distribution unspecified, so results reproduce poorly. A recent system, AutoEval, automates both reset and scoring, but only for single-step tasks, because a long-horizon rollout can terminate in combinatorially many configurations that no single learned reset policy covers. We present HALTER, a Harness for Autonomous Long-horizon Task Evaluation and Reset, which restores the scene by planning over a library of learned atomic reset skills, so demonstration cost scales with the size of that library rather than with the number of terminal states. HALTER builds a spatial scene graph online from point clouds and vision foundation models, and an LLM reasons over this graph to score the rollout, plan the reset, and verify that the reset succeeded, without collecting labeled success images for any task. On four long-horizon tasks on a Franka arm, HALTER restores the scene in 76% of episodes, against 52% for AutoEval and 65% for a motion-planning reset, and it estimates the completed-skill fraction correctly in 90% of episodes, against 76%. Its reset-verification verdict is correct in 91% of episodes, compared with 78% for AutoEval. It also cuts the operator time of an evaluation campaign by 72% relative to manual reset. We further measure compositional generalization on three held-out tasks, where HALTER resets 74.7% of episodes against 1.3% for a per-task reset policy, and we ablate the scene representation and the graph update rate.

</details>


### [120] [Predict Before You Deploy: Offline Prediction of Quantization-Induced Task Degradation for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19441)
*Jiuyi Xu,Jinjia Guo,Meida Chen,Jing Du,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 PreDE 框架，通过离线动作偏差预测量化对世界动作模型(WAM)任务性能的影响，在部署前对量化配置进行接受/拒绝/推迟决策，减少昂贵的闭环评估成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型(WAM)依赖视频生成骨干网络，部署需要大量内存和算力。训练后量化可降低内存并加速推理，但位宽、分组、量化器等构成巨大的配置空间。通过穷举闭环评估来寻找能保持任务性能的配置成本极高。

Method: 提出 PreDE(部署前预测)，一个策略校准框架，从离线动作偏差预测量化引起的任务退化。利用小型开发集的闭环结果校准两个阈值，使用固定观测日志对新配置进行接受、拒绝或推迟决策。在"设定内标签排序假设"下，规则在所有与开发标签一致的阈值处给出一致决策。

Result: 在五种 WAM 和四种基准设定中，量化导致的任务损失因配置而异，无法仅由位宽或共享偏差阈值解释。在两策略的 28 个留出配置中，PreDE 在未观测闭环结果前给出 21 个决策(75%覆盖率)，全部与观测标签一致。在 Franka Research 3 上 450 次试验中，高偏差组配置均显著退化，低偏差组无显著退化。真机上 W4A4 实现 1.37 倍动作查询加速和约 44% 峰值内存降低。

Conclusion: 结果支持针对策略特定的行为校准用于量化配置选择，同时能识别需要闭环评估的候选配置。代码已开源。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) rely on video-generation backbones, requiring substantial memory and compute for deployment. Post-training quantization reduces memory and can accelerate inference, but bit width, grouping, and quantizer choice define a large configuration space. Identifying configurations that preserve task performance through exhaustive closed-loop evaluation is costly. We propose PreDE (Predict Before You Deploy), a policy-calibrated framework for predicting quantization-induced task degradation from offline action deviations. Using closed-loop outcomes from a small development set, PreDE calibrates two thresholds and accepts, rejects, or defers new configurations using a fixed observation log. Under a within-setting label-ordering hypothesis, the rule issues decisions where all thresholds consistent with the development labels agree. Across five WAMs and four benchmark settings, quantization produces configuration-dependent task losses that cannot be explained by bit width alone or a shared deviation threshold. Across 28 held-out configurations from two policies, PreDE issued 21 decisions before observing closed-loop outcomes (75% coverage), all matching the observed acceptable or degraded labels. Deferred candidates included both acceptable outcomes and a 33-percentage-point loss. In 450 Franka Research 3 trials across two independently fine-tuned policies, all configurations assigned to high-deviation groups before testing showed significant degradation, while low-deviation comparisons showed no statistically significant degradation. On the real robot, W4A4 achieved a 1.37x action-query speedup and approximately 44% lower peak memory. These results support policy-specific behavioral calibration for quantization configuration selection while identifying candidates that require closed-loop evaluation. The code is available at https://github.com/jiuyixu25/PreDE.

</details>


### [121] [From Wizard-of-Oz Human-Robot Dialogue Collection to a Taxonomy of Robot Response Decisions: A Retrospective Analysis of Assistive Pilot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19447)
*Guangping Liu,Nicholas Hawkins,Tipu Sultan,Flavio Esposito,Madi Dian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过复盘一项室内机器人任务的Wizard-of-Oz试点研究，从40个episode中提炼出一套用于机器人回应决策的层级分类体系，包括6种回应模式和4类歧义类型，并验证了其标注一致性与用于训练视觉语言模型的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常室内环境中跟随自然语言指令的机器人常需应对不完整的人类话语，指令可能缺失关键信息，如不可见物体的身份、目标地点或用户意图。现有数据集缺乏真实世界的情境对话，也少有基于实践的标准来判断机器人何时应行动、确认、澄清或拒绝。作者希望建立明确的决策方案来解决这一问题。

Method: 作者回顾性分析了一项Wizard-of-Oz试点研究，5名参与者使用轮椅搭载的移动操作臂完成开门、开抽屉、喂食、饮水、清洁等日常室内任务，wizard在无正式沟通策略的情况下进行回应。基于40个episode，作者推导出包含6种回应模式（ANSWER、REPORT_DONE、REFUSE、CONFIRM、CLARIFY、ACT）和4类歧义类型（intent、referential、spatial、intelligibility）的层级分类体系。两名人类标注者和一名AI标注者将该体系应用于试点数据，并对LLaVA-1.6-7B进行了微调。

Result: 两名人类标注者和一名AI标注者应用该体系后，干净标签率分别为91%和89%，在决策点、模式和歧义层面上，人类-人类及人类-AI比较的Cohen's kappa值范围为0.72至0.95。对LLaVA-1.6-7B在ACT和CLARIFY的分类标签上进行微调，表明使用该分类体系的标注来训练视觉语言模型是可行的。在决策点识别和REPORT_DONE方面仍存在边界案例。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的层级分类体系和决策方案为机器人在不完整指令下的回应决策提供了实践性标准，并验证了其标注可靠性与训练视觉语言模型的可行性。剩余的边界案例表明需要一个受约束的协议来实现更一致的对话收集。

Abstract: Robots that follow natural-language instructions in everyday indoor environments must act on incomplete human utterances. Instructions often omit essential information, such as the identity of an out-of-view object, an intended destination, or the user's goal. Existing datasets contain little real-world situated dialogue and provide few practice-grounded criteria for deciding when a robot should act, confirm, clarify, or refuse. We retrospectively analyze a pilot Wizard-of-Oz study in which five participants performed everyday indoor tasks, including door opening, drawer opening, feeding, drinking, and cleaning, with a wheelchair-mounted mobile manipulator while the wizard responded without a formal communication policy. This preserved authentic user behavior but produced inconsistent robot-side decisions, motivating an explicit decision scheme. From 40 episodes, we derived a hierarchical taxonomy of six response modes (ANSWER, REPORT_DONE, REFUSE, CONFIRM, CLARIFY, ACT) and four ambiguity types (intent, referential, spatial, intelligibility). Two human annotators and an AI annotator applied the scheme to the pilot data. Clean-label rates were 91% and 89%, and Cohen's ranged from 0.72 to 0.95 across decision-point, mode, and ambiguity levels for both human-human and human-AI comparisons. Fine-tuning LLaVA-1.6-7B on taxonomy-derived labels for ACT and CLARIFY indicates the feasibility of training vision-language models using annotations from our taxonomy. Remaining boundary cases in decision-point identification and REPORT_DONE motivate a constrained protocol for more consistent dialogue collection.

</details>


### [122] [Winning a Won Game: Strict Reach-Avoid-Stay Control Barrier Functions for High-Dimensional Black-Box Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19449)
*Donggeon David Oh,Duy P. Nguyen,Gongkai Yuan,Qingchen Li,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Haimin Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向高维黑盒系统、在有限不确定性下的严格到达-保持（sRAS）Q控制屏障函数（CBF）安全滤波器，结合"保持值"与"到达-规避值"构造鲁棒离散时间CBF，并提升为状态-动作Q函数用于运行时干预，无需已知动力学或手工屏障，在四足机器人跳跃与F1TENTH竞速中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人不仅需要完成任务，还必须在进入目标后持续保持成果，并始终避免安全失效。现有方法难以在仅有黑盒交互、存在有界不确定性且系统高维的条件下同时保证"安全到达"与"永久保持"，因此需要一种形式化、可扩展的安全滤波方案。

Method: 提出sRAS Q-CBF安全滤波器：构造"保持值"以编码在目标子集中的安全永久驻留，构造"到达-规避值"以编码安全到达该子集并规避无法保证安全驻留的目标状态；证明二者联合构成有效的鲁棒离散时间CBF，并将其提升为状态-动作Q函数用于运行时干预；采用基于可达性的对抗强化学习进行可扩展的值近似，仅依赖黑盒交互。

Result: 在精确值及测度为零的条件下，滤波器可从几乎所有可获胜初始状态保持sRAS可行性，并在首次进入后始终使系统安全停留在目标内，抵御所有容许的不确定性实现。四足机器人间隙跳跃仿真与硬件实验实现了跨越、安全着陆并保持安全；F1TENTH竞速仿真展示了安全超车与保持领先。

Conclusion: 该方法为高维黑盒系统提供了无需已知动力学、仿射结构、值导数或手工屏障的sRAS安全保障，兼顾理论鲁棒性保证与实际可扩展性，并在仿真与硬件中得到验证。

Abstract: Robots must complete their tasks and maintain the achieved outcomes while avoiding safety failures at all times. Strict reach-avoid-stay (sRAS) formalizes this requirement: safely reaching a target and remaining there indefinitely after first entry. We propose an sRAS Q-control barrier function (CBF) safety filter for high-dimensional black-box systems under bounded uncertainty. Our construction combines a stay value encoding safe permanent residence in a target subset with a reach-avoid value encoding safe reachability of this subset while avoiding target states from which safe permanent residence cannot be guaranteed. We prove that these values jointly yield a valid robust discrete-time CBF and lift them to state-action Q-functions for runtime intervention. For exact values and under a measure-zero condition, our filter preserves sRAS feasibility from almost every winnable initial state and keeps the system safely within the target after first entry, against all admissible uncertainty realizations. We adopt reachability-based adversarial reinforcement learning for scalable value approximation using only black-box interactions. Notably, neither synthesis nor deployment of our filter requires known dynamics, affine structure, value derivatives, or hand-designed barriers. We validate our framework in quadruped gap jumping in simulation and hardware, where the robot crosses the gap, lands safely, and remains safe afterward. Simulated F1TENTH races further demonstrate safe overtaking and lead retention.

</details>


### [123] [GLAMDRING: Gait Learning And Morphology co-Design via Reinforcement LearnING of CPGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19452)
*Amogh Joshi,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GLAMDRING框架，针对给定运动任务规格，联合合成最优四足机器人形态（连杆几何与关节执行器）并学习驱动其运动的CPG步态控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人正从结构化工厂走向灾难现场、行星表面、农田等非结构化环境，而适配这些场景的合适机器人往往尚不存在，因此需要能针对特定运动任务自动设计机器人本体与控制器的联合方法。

Method: 输入前进速度上下界、单执行器功率预算、执行器库与负载要求，输出匹配的四足形态与基于Hopf振荡器的CPG步态策略；按最大速度、最小运输成本或最大负载裕度等目标对可行设计排序；通过在候选形态空间中用强化学习训练少量CPG策略实现本体与步态协同学习，再由策略记录的运行包线事后解析连杆长度与执行器，将合成成本降为固定的小规模RL运行数。

Result: 实验得出三点关键结论：满足运动约束必须协同设计本体与步态；决定可实现负载能力的是执行器包线可行性而非单纯运动成功；在多数设计中，仅凭形态与约束即可自然涌现出典型动物步态。并进行了真实世界演示验证有效性。

Conclusion: GLAMDRING能以较低计算成本联合优化机器人形态与步态控制，在非结构化环境中针对运动任务自动生成可行且高效的四足机器人设计，并已在实物演示中验证。

Abstract: Robots are moving out of the structured factory floor and into unstructured environments such as disaster sites, planetary surfaces, and agricultural fields, for which the right robot often does not yet exist. We present GLAMDRING, a framework that synthesizes the optimal robot for a locomotion task and, jointly, learns the controller that drives it. For the given specifications of forward-velocity bounds, a per-actuator power budget, an actuator library, and a payload requirement, GLAMDRING returns a matched quadruped morphology (link geometry and per-joint actuators) and a Hopf-oscillator Central Pattern Generator (CPG) gait policy. We rank feasible designs against a target design objective, viz., maximum speed, minimum Cost of Transport (CoT), or max Payload Margin. Because body and locomotion are coupled, the optimal morphology dictates how a robot is driven, while optimal gait depends on the physical body. We train a small number of CPG policies by reinforcement learning across the space of candidate morphologies, co-learning the gait with the underlying robot hardware. Link lengths and actuators are then resolved post-hoc from the policy's logged operating envelope, reducing synthesis cost to a small, fixed number of reinforcement-learning runs instead of one per candidate. Our experiments show three key findings: co-designing body and gait is necessary to satisfy locomotion constraints; actuator-envelope feasibility, rather than locomotion success alone, determines realizable payload capacity; and canonical animal gaits emerge naturally in most designs from morphology and constraints alone. A real-world demonstration further highlights the efficacy of our work.

</details>


### [124] [Pose-aware Legged Robot Semantic Exploration with Omnidirectional Perception in Confined Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19460)
*Xiaoyang Zhan,Shiyu Chen,Kenji Shimada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POSE：一种姿态感知的语义探索系统，利用腿足机器人身体俯仰/翻滚与全向相机-LiDAR感知，结合VLM辅助的视点剪枝，在受限环境中提升目标表面覆盖并缩短探索时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限环境中语义探索需兼顾环境建图与目标物体细节观测。地面机器人受传感器垂直视场和站位距离限制，从平面视角难以观测物体上部表面；身体倾斜可提升覆盖，但额外观测与姿态切换会增加任务时间，需解决这一覆盖-效率权衡。

Method: 提出姿态感知视点采样模块，基于部分物体地图按期望覆盖增益选择身体姿态，并通过对准执行减少不必要的身体重定向；引入以物体为中心的视点剪枝策略，借助视觉语言模型（VLM），利用持续观测历史与鸟瞰（BEV）地图减少冗余检查访问；将语义视点与几何探索视点结合于全局探索规划器中。

Result: 仿真显示：相比平面规划基线，最终目标表面覆盖率提升8-10个百分点，探索时间减少17-32%，并在所评估基线中获得最高的平均物体覆盖AUC。真实机器车间实验中，搭载全向相机-LiDAR套件的腿足机器人验证了系统适用性。

Conclusion: 结果表明自适应身体姿态规划可改善腿足机器人语义探索中的覆盖-效率权衡；作者计划未来开源代码以惠及社区。

Abstract: Semantic exploration in confined environments requires both environment mapping and detailed observation of target objects. For ground robots, limited sensor vertical fields of view and restricted standoff distances can leave upper object surfaces unobserved from planar viewpoints. Body tilting can improve coverage, but additional observations and posture transitions increase mission time. To address this trade-off, we present POSE, a pose-aware semantic exploration system that exploits a legged robot's intrinsic body pitch and roll with omnidirectional camera-LiDAR perception. The proposed pose-aware viewpoint sampling module selects body postures from partial object maps according to expected coverage gain, while aim-aligned execution reduces unnecessary body reorientation. Further, we introduce an object-centric viewpoint pruning strategy assisted by a vision-language model (VLM), which uses persistent observation history and bird's-eye-view (BEV) maps to reduce redundant inspection visits. The resulting semantic viewpoints are combined with geometric exploration viewpoints in a global exploration planner. Simulations show that POSE improves final target-surface coverage by 8-10 percentage points over the planar planning baseline while reducing exploration time by 17-32%, and achieves the highest mean object coverage AUC among the evaluated baselines. Real-world experiments with a legged robot carrying an omnidirectional camera-LiDAR suite in a machine shop further demonstrate the system's applicability. These results support adaptive body-posture planning for improving the coverage-efficiency trade-off in legged robot semantic exploration. We plan to release the code for community benefit in the future.

</details>


### [125] [FASA: Feedback-Aware Sampling Adaptation for Efficient Diffusion-Based VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19475)
*Yuchen Han,Jianhan Wu,Xiaoyang Qu,Lingwei Kong,Shiyi Li,Jianzong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FASA，一种免训练的运行时框架，利用实时多模态反馈动态调控扩散VLA模型的去噪采样，实现最高1.45倍推理加速并保持成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式VLA模型在具身任务中性能优异，但迭代采样带来高昂计算与访存开销，难以在边缘平台实时部署。现有加速方法需昂贵训练（蒸馏、流匹配），或依赖静态剪枝与缓存而损害感知，忽视了机器人交互中动态的工作负载变化。

Method: FASA将实时多模态反馈视为去噪流程的控制信号：一是交互驱动的范围适配器，依据视觉与夹爪力反馈调节全局采样步数预算；二是本体感知步适配器，在调整后的范围内定位最优步骤。二者协同使底层硬件架构自适应匹配不同执行阶段的工作负载需求。整个框架无需训练。

Result: 在多个基准上的对比评估显示，推理速度最高提升1.45倍，同时保持有竞争力的成功率。

Conclusion: FASA为在资源受限计算平台上部署重型生成式具身AI工作负载提供了新的动态运行时架构范式。

Abstract: Diffusion-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong performance in embodied tasks, but their iterative sampling imposes heavy computational and memory-access cost, blocking real-time deployment on edge platforms. Existing acceleration methods either require expensive training (e.g., distillation, flow matching) or degrade perception via statically scheduled pruning and caching, ignoring the dynamic workload variance of robotic interactions. This paper presents FASA (Feedback-Aware Sampling Adaptation), a training-free runtime framework that treats real-time multimodal feedback as a control signal for the denoising pipeline: an interaction-driven range adaptor modulates the global sampling-step budget based on visual and gripper-force feedback, and a proprioception-aware step adaptor pinpoints the optimized step within the adapted range. This co-designed framework allows the underlying hardware architecture to adaptively match the workload demands of different execution phases. Comparative evaluations across several benchmarks show that the inference speed can be increased by up to 1.45$\times$ while maintaining competitive success rates, providing a novel dynamic runtime architecture paradigm for deploying heavy generative embodied AI workloads onto resource-constrained computing platforms.

</details>


### [126] [Self-excited actuation enables adaptive and resilient flapping-wing flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19480)
*Rundong Yang,Ethan S. Wold,Ellen Liu,James Lynch,Wei Zhou,Mark Jankauski,Simon Sponberg,Nick Gravish*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了首个可飞行的异步驱动扑翼机器人，证明异步驱动能让翅膀无需控制输入即可响应共振力学变化并瞬时应对碰撞，在杂乱环境中比同步驱动更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 昆虫飞行肌肉分为同步肌与异步肌，异步肌具有内在的拉伸激活响应，可自发产生振翅而无需大脑信号，被认为为飞行控制提供了自适应与快速响应能力。然而现有飞行机器人多采用同步驱动，异步驱动的机器人潜力尚未被验证。

Method: 研究者构建了首个采用异步驱动的可飞行扑翼机器人，并将异步驱动与同步驱动进行对比，在杂乱环境中开展飞行测试。

Result: 异步驱动使翅膀无需控制输入即可响应身体共振力学的变化，且无需外部传感即可瞬时响应与障碍物的碰撞；在杂乱环境飞行测试中，异步驱动相比同步驱动显著提升了稳定性与性能。

Conclusion: 模拟飞行昆虫异步肌肉的扑翼机器人驱动策略，能在控制系统介入之前提供快速、反应式的驱动响应。将体现式控制分配至低层驱动动力学与高层感知运动系统，为新型飞行机器人提供了有吸引力的蓝图。

Abstract: The muscles that power insect flight fall into one of two categories: 1) synchronous muscles that contract under direct control from the nervous system, and 2) asynchronous muscles which have an intrinsic stretch activation response that spontaneously generates wingbeats without the need for signaling from the brain. It is thought that the emergent nature of asynchronous wingbeats provides both adaptive and responsive capabilities for flight control. To date, most flying robots use synchronous actuation. In this paper we develop the first flight-capable flapping wing robot that uses asynchronous actuation. We demonstrate that asynchronous actuation allows wings to respond to changes in the resonant mechanics of the body without control input, and wings can react instantaneously to collisions with obstacles with no extrinsic sensing needed. Flight tests within cluttered environments demonstrate that asynchronous actuation significantly improves stability and performance when compared to synchronous actuation. In total this work demonstrates that a flapping wing robot actuation strategy that emulates the asynchronous muscles of flying insects can provide fast, reactive actuation responses before a control system would need to intervene. This partitioning of embodied control to both the low-level actuation dynamics and and high-level sensorimotor system provides a compelling blueprint for new flying robots.

</details>


### [127] [PIVOT: Perception-aware Independent Viewpoint Online Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19510)
*Yuyang Chen,Shekoufeh Sadeghi,Charuvahan Adhivarahan,Elton Lemos,Chen Wang,Sanjeev J. Koppal,Karthik Dantu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PIVOT，一种轻量迭代方法，在固定平移轨迹下优化运动解耦传感器的视线方向以最大化特征可见性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人感知假设传感器视场相对机体固定，而云台相机、MEMS激光雷达等运动解耦传感器可在运行时独立控制感知方向。这种自由度带来了计算挑战：如何在特征密集环境中在线高效选择有用的视角。

Method: 在圆锥视场模型下，可见性仅取决于光轴，从而将问题化为视角球面S^2上的二自由度优化。采用无坐标的SO(3)指数映射更新，实现高效连续优化，无需显式角度参数化或穷举视角球搜索。

Result: 蒙特卡洛评估显示，方法保留了暴力搜索可见性的98.1%–99.6%，同时获得76–85倍加速。逼真仿真与真实世界实验进一步证明，视觉定位鲁棒性提升且视角控制实用，并在四足机器人上得到验证。

Conclusion: PIVOT为运动解耦传感器提供了高效、轻量的在线视角优化方案，在保持近乎最优可见性的同时大幅提速，并具备实用的真机部署能力。

Abstract: A fundamental assumption in robotic perception is that the sensor's field of view (FoV) is fixed relative to the robot body. Motion-decoupled sensors, such as gimbal-mounted cameras and MEMS-based LiDARs, instead allow sensing direction to be controlled independently at runtime. This freedom creates a computational challenge: efficiently selecting useful viewing directions online in feature-dense environments. We propose PIVOT, a lightweight iterative method that optimizes sensor viewing direction along a fixed translation trajectory to maximize feature visibility. Under a conical FoV model, visibility depends only on the optical axis, yielding a two-degree-of-freedom optimization on the viewing sphere $S^2$. Coordinate-free $SO(3)$ exponential-map updates enable efficient continuous optimization without explicit angular parameterizations or exhaustive viewing-sphere search. Monte Carlo evaluations retain 98.1--99.6% of brute-force visibility with a 76--85x speedup. Photorealistic simulation and real-world experiments further demonstrate improved visual localization robustness and practical viewpoint control on a quadruped robot.

</details>


### [128] [CoreSense: Traceable Failure Recall and Conflict-Aware Belief Gating for Auditable Robot Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19512)
*Zoe Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoreSense是一种机器人系统集成架构，通过可追溯的情景证据、冲突感知信念门与有界可审计建议，在允许PROCEED、请求重新观察、弃权或升级前，对范围、来源、时间、矛盾与支持度进行检查，从而降低不安全继续执行的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人可以回忆先前失败，但无法判断这些回忆的证据是否仍然有效、是否与当前观察冲突、或是否足以支撑决策，因此需要一种机制来对这些不确定性进行把关。

Method: 提出CoreSense架构，结合可追溯情景证据、冲突感知信念门与有界可审计建议；门控检查范围、来源、时间、矛盾、支持五个维度，输出PROCEED、请求重新观察、弃权或升级；评测分三层：离线公共真实机器人数据、冻结的信号级仿真、以及实时云部署路径。

Result: 在CableTrace-120与BotFails-200上，信念门控将协议定义的不安全继续执行从20%和40%降至0%；原始视频策略也达到0%不安全继续但阻塞全部标称回合；ViFailback-BotFails视觉检测器AUROC为0.778但仍为全阻塞；cycle-disjoint UR3遥测实现0%不安全继续、36.1%过度阻塞、61.9%覆盖率，抓取丢失迁移为负结果；受控物理验证为3.3%、0%、42.0%，冲突感知融合为4.7%、0%、42.8%；20/20云端回忆验证了CockroachDB Cloud-Amazon Bedrock部署路径。

Conclusion: 证据支持一种可审计的集成模式，而非自主恢复或经认证的安全性。

Abstract: Robots can recall prior failures without knowing whether recalled evidence remains valid, conflicts with current observations, or is sufficient to guide a decision. We present CoreSense, a robot-system integration architecture that combines traceable episodic evidence with a conflict-aware belief gate and bounded, auditable recommendations. The gate checks scope, provenance, time, contradiction, and support before it permits PROCEED, requests re-observation, abstains, or escalates. Evaluation follows three complementary layers without commanding a physical robot: offline public real-robot data, a frozen signal-level simulation, and a live cloud deployment path. On CableTrace-120 and BotFails-200, belief gating reduces protocol-defined unsafe proceeds from 20% and 40% to 0%. A disjointly calibrated raw-video policy also reaches 0% unsafe proceed, but overblocks every nominal episode. On public data, a ViFailback-BotFails visual detector reaches 0.778 AUROC yet remains all-blocking, whereas cycle-disjoint UR3 telemetry for protective stops yields 0% unsafe proceed, 36.1% overblocking, and 61.9% coverage; grip-loss transfer remains a negative result. Controlled physical corroboration yields 3.3%, 0%, and 42.0%, while conflict-aware fusion yields 4.7%, 0%, and 42.8%. Finally, 20/20 cloud recalls validate a CockroachDB Cloud-Amazon Bedrock deployment path. The evidence supports an auditable integration pattern, not autonomous recovery or certified safety.

</details>


### [129] [AURORA: A Natural Language-Driven Agentic Framework for Understanding, Reasoning, and Orchestrating Reliable Air-Ground Co-Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19527)
*Keshu Wu,Hao Zhang,Rui Gan,Xiangbo Gao,Xiaopeng Li,Zhengzhong Tu,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自然语言驱动的智能体框架 AURORA，将空地场景生成视为“编译+验证”过程，引入类型化中间表示 AGSG，实现解析、落地、时序规划、可行性检查、运行时验证与局部修复，并构建 AURORA-Bench 评估生成场景是否忠实实现所需交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地交通研究日益依赖联合仿真，但场景构建耗时且难以验证。更关键的是，生成场景可能执行成功却未实现用户要求的时间、空间、通信或行为关系，即存在“静默失败”。

Method: AURORA 以自然语言为驱动，核心是 Air-Ground Scenario Graph（AGSG）这一类型化中间表示，显式连接智能体、空中任务、事件、通信链路、成功条件及其跨域依赖；并支持模拟器落地解析、路空联合落地、时序规划、执行前可行性检查、基于轨迹的运行时验证、失败定位与有界修复。同时提出 AURORA-Bench 基准。

Result: 跨多个语言模型的实验表明：结构化执行显著提升可靠性；运行时验证能暴露基于完成度的评估所忽略的静默失败；局部修复可在不重新生成整个场景的情况下解决许多违规。

Conclusion: 可靠的场景生成需要验证实际实现的行为，而非仅验证可执行代码，体现了显式中间表示在可验证、可修复的语言驱动联合仿真中的价值。

Abstract: Air-ground transportation research increasingly relies on co-simulation, yet constructing scenarios remains labor-intensive and difficult to validate. More importantly, a generated scenario may execute successfully while failing to realize the spatial, temporal, communication, or behavioral relationships requested by the user. This paper presents AURORA, a natural-language-driven agentic framework that treats air-ground scenario generation as a process of compilation with verification. Central to AURORA is the Air-Ground Scenario Graph (AGSG), a typed intermediate representation that explicitly connects agents, aerial missions, events, communication links, success conditions, and their cross-domain dependencies. This shared representation enables simulator-grounded parsing, joint road-airspace grounding, temporal planning, pre-execution feasibility checking, trace-based runtime verification, failure localization, and bounded repair within a unified workflow. We further introduce AURORA-Bench to evaluate not only whether generated scenarios execute, but whether they faithfully realize the requested interactions. Experiments across multiple language models show that structured execution substantially improves reliability, while runtime verification exposes silent failures that completion-based evaluation overlooks. Localized repair further resolves many violations without regenerating the entire scenario. The results show that reliable scenario generation requires verifying realized behavior, not merely executable code, and demonstrate the value of explicit intermediate representations for verifiable and repairable language-driven co-simulation.

</details>


### [130] [SLAMSqueezeBench: Comparing SLAM Systems under Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19533)
*Mohamed Hefny,Karthik Dantu,Steven Y. Ko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SLAMSqueezeBench，一个可在边缘硬件上对SLAM系统施加计算与内存约束、并模拟真实相机丢帧的测试框架，用于在真实资源约束下公平比较不同SLAM系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLAM系统多在隔离环境下构建和测试，性能报告仿佛是其独占系统资源。边缘硬件资源受限，SLAM需与其他任务协同运行，但缺少在真实资源约束下比较SLAM系统的通用机制/基准。

Method: 开发SLAMSqueezeBench框架：在SLAM执行过程中对可用的计算和内存资源施加约束；通过有限缓冲区装满时丢帧的方式，模拟真实的相机帧采集过程。基于该框架比较了九种SLAM系统，涵盖经典方法、基于学习的方法以及高斯splatting方法。

Result: 利用SLAMSqueezeBench对九种SLAM系统（经典、学习型、高斯splatting）进行了对比测试；测试框架将在论文发表后向社区开放使用。

Conclusion: SLAMSqueezeBench填补了SLAM系统在真实资源约束下比较机制的空白，为边缘硬件上的SLAM评测提供了统一手段，并将向社区开放。

Abstract: Simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) is one of the services running on an autonomous robot. It is typically run to assist other tasks such as planning, manipulation, etc. All these tasks are run on edge hardware and are subject to severe resource constraints. However, most SLAM systems are built and tested in isolation, and their performance is reported as if they are the only task running on a system. We observe that existing benchmarks lack a common mechanism for comparing SLAM systems under realistic resource constraints. To address this limitation, we have developed SLAMSqueezeBench, a framework that allows testing of SLAM systems under realistic workloads on edge hardware. It does so by imposing constraints on compute and memory resources available for the SLAM system during execution. It also simulates realistic camera frame acquisition with frame drops when a finite buffer is full. Using SLAMSqueezeBench, we compare nine SLAM systems spanning classical systems, learning-based systems, and approaches for Gaussian splatting. Our testing framework will be available for use by the community upon publication.

</details>


### [131] [Navigate or Relocate? Planning Among Movable Obstacles in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19541)
*Yuqing Zhang,Haoyu Zhu,Yiannis Kantaros*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究未知环境下的可移动障碍物导航（NAMO）问题，提出一种在线框架，通过在处理可移动物体时权衡"绕行"与"搬移"来决策，并利用采样方法与LLM引导搜索相互依赖的搬移序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人规划假设存在无碰撞路径，但当所有路径被阻塞时，机器人必须决定搬移哪些物体、以何顺序、放置何处。现有NAMO规划器多假设环境已知；针对未知环境的方法通常只做局部搬移推理，无法规划相互依赖的搬移序列。因此需要一种能在未知环境中在线决策导航还是搬移、并能规划依赖序列的方法。

Method: 提出在线框架，将已发现的可移动物体分别视为障碍或可移除物，通过最短路径比较来消解"路径被阻塞需搬移"还是"未探索空间可能存在通路"的歧义，从而在导航与搬移间选择。导航复用现有运动规划器；搬移采用基于采样的方法，搜索相互依赖的搬移序列，并用LLM对采样进行偏置。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法可扩展到需要相互依赖搬移的杂乱环境，并且相比现有基线在规划质量上有提升。

Conclusion: 针对未知环境下的NAMO问题，在线框架结合最短路径歧义消解、采样式依赖搬移序列搜索与LLM偏置，实现了在杂乱环境中的可扩展性与更优的规划质量，优于现有基线。

Abstract: Conventional robot planning methods seek collision-free paths to a goal but fail when all paths are blocked. In these cases, the robot must determine which objects to relocate, in what order, and where to place them to clear a path---a problem known as Navigation Among Movable Obstacles (NAMO). Most NAMO planners assume a known environment, while existing approaches for unknown environments typically reason locally about relocations and cannot plan interdependent relocation sequences. We consider NAMO in unknown environments revealed through onboard sensing, where the robot must decide whether a blocked route requires relocation or a feasible path may exist through unexplored space. We propose an online framework that addresses this ambiguity by selecting between navigation and relocation using shortest paths that treat discovered movable objects as obstacles or as removable. Navigation relies on existing motion planners, while relocation uses a sampling-based approach that, unlike existing approaches for unknown environments, searches over \textit{interdependent} relocation sequences and uses an LLM to bias sampling. Numerical experiments demonstrate scalability to cluttered environments requiring interdependent relocations and improved plan quality over existing baselines.

</details>


### [132] [VABench: Measuring Embodied Spatial Intelligence through Visual Demonstrations, Active Perception, and Metric Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19554)
*Zhongbo Zhang,Jiayi Jin,Yifan Wang,Zaibin Zhang,Haiwen Diao,Lijun Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出VA-Bench基准，评估多模态大模型在空间智能中"观察-推理-行动-修正"的完整闭环能力，涵盖主动视角选择、度量命令生成与执行反馈修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间智能不仅需要描述物体位置，还需在不完整观测下识别并主动获取缺失证据、在统一空间框架中解读证据并据此行动。现有基准多为被动观测，未考察模型主动采集证据、发出度量动作命令并根据反馈修正的完整闭环能力。

Method: 构建VA-Bench基准：包含14个基础任务族（11个单臂、3个双臂）、7个留出几何/布局变体，以及长时程五物体组合赛道。模型仅从RGB演示学习程序性上下文，主动选择相机视角、发出笛卡尔度量命令，并依据执行反馈修正；不提供特权物体位姿、oracle轨迹或学习到的动作头。由与模型无关的固定控制器仅执行模型指定目标。评估12个主要模型条件，每个基础任务使用20个物理验证种子做三次独立运行，报告终端成功率、9项轨迹级行为诊断和子任务进度。

Result: 最佳模型在标注运行中目标定位达100.0%、空间关系达78.9%，但三次运行宏平均任务成功率仅53.93±3.17%。主动相机控制显著优于被动多视图观测，匹配对比中成功率从27.86%提升至57.50%。留出几何迁移可使成功率下降超30个百分点。无模型能完成严格的长时程episode，尽管有相当的部分进展。

Conclusion: VA-Bench用于检验通用多模态大模型能否将视觉演示与主动获取的证据转化为成功的具身行动；结果表明模型在完整闭环、泛化与长时程任务上仍有明显不足。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence requires more than describing object locations. Under incomplete observation, models must identify and acquire missing evidence, interpret it in a common spatial frame, and act on it. We introduce VA-Bench to evaluate the complete observe-reason-act-revise loop. General-purpose MLLMs learn procedural context from RGB-only demonstrations, actively select camera viewpoints, issue metric Cartesian commands, and revise them from execution feedback. Models receive no privileged object poses, oracle trajectories, or learned action heads. A fixed model-agnostic controller executes only model-specified targets. VA-Bench contains 14 base task families (11 single-arm and three dual-arm), seven held-out geometry/layout variants, and a long-horizon five-object composition track. We evaluate 12 primary model conditions in three independent runs over the same 20 physically verified seeds per base task, reporting terminal success, nine trajectory-level behavioral diagnostics, and subtask progress. First, the best-performing model scores 100.0% on target localization and 78.9% on spatial relations in the annotated run. Its three-run macro-average task success is only 53.93+/-3.17%. Second, active camera control significantly improves task success over passive multi-view observation. In one matched comparison, success rises from 27.86% to 57.50%. Third, held-out geometric transfer can reduce task success by over 30 percentage points. No model completes a strict long-horizon episode, despite substantial partial progress. VA-Bench thus tests whether general-purpose MLLMs can turn visual demonstrations and actively acquired evidence into successful embodied action.

</details>


### [133] [Tele-Traversability: Rethinking Traversability for Teleoperated Ground Robots in Terrain Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19577)
*Lewei Feng,Qi Chen,Wenshuo Wang,Xianghao Meng,Minh Kieu,Haijie Guan,Jin Wu,Fuchun Sun,Junqiang Xi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"遥操作可通行性(tele-traversability)"概念，将可通行性评估从以机器人为中心扩展到以人为中心，纳入操作员的认知状态（注意力、工作负荷、风险容忍度等），以解决遥操作中人与机器人可通行性评估不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作中，人通过人机界面利用离场、不完整且有延迟的反馈做判断，而机器人依赖现场车载传感信息评估可通行性，二者易产生分歧，导致决策与动作不匹配。现有可通行性建模、估计与预测方法多从机器人视角出发，适用于全自主机器人，却忽视了人类操作员的影响。

Method: 本文属于观点/综述性工作：首先重新审视机器人学中可通行性的定义与作用，然后将其扩展至遥操作场景，提出以人为中心的"tele-traversability"概念，将操作员的认知状态（注意力、工作负荷、风险容忍或风险意识）纳入考量。

Result: 文章系统梳理了可通行性概念从机器人中心到人类中心的演变，界定了遥操作情境下可通行性的新内涵，并指出未来趋势与开放挑战。

Conclusion: 面向以人为中心的遥操作系统，tele-traversability为解决人机可通行性评估不一致提供了新方向，未来需进一步研究认知状态建模及人机对齐等开放问题。

Abstract: Teleoperation, a human-in-the-loop control scheme, allows a human operator to remotely command and guide a mobile robot to navigate in off-road environments, yet fluent and user-friendly tele-navigation requires an alignment of traversability evaluation between human and robot. In the teleoperation system, the human operator typically utilizes off-site incomplete and delayed feedback via a human-machine interface to make a judgment of traversability, while the robot makes such an evaluation based on in situ onboard sensory information, which could cause divergent traversability estimation and thus generate mismatched decisions and actions. Existing approaches for traversability modeling, estimation, and prediction are mainly derived from the view of robots, i.e., robot-centric, and are practically suitable for fully autonomous mobile robots, but neglect the influence of human operators. To address this problem, this paper extends the concept of traversability from robot-centric to human-centric by accounting for the operator's cognitive states, such as attention, workload, and risk tolerance or awareness, termed tele-traversability. We first revisit the definitions and roles of traversability in robotics and then extend them to teleoperation settings. Finally, we highlight future trends and open challenges of tele-traversability toward human-centric teleoperation systems.

</details>


### [134] [Recovering Aggressively Pruned Vision-Language-Action Models with Offline Hidden-State Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19579)
*Chiyoung Kim,Sanghyuk Roy Choi,Minhyeok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 宽度剪枝结合隐藏状态蒸馏可在完全离线条件下恢复大幅压缩的VLA模型性能，约8 GPU小时即可接近教师水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型数十亿参数的语言主干难以部署到机器人硬件；结构化剪枝虽能压缩，但强力剪枝（如63%）会导致LIBERO-Long成功率从93.2%骤降至0.8%。已有恢复方法依赖监督微调加强化学习，需要在线采样和数百GPU小时，成本高。

Method: 采用宽度剪枝：缩窄模块但保持残差流原始尺寸，使教师与学生隐藏状态形状一致，可直接匹配而无需投影器。学生针对教师单次前向构建的缓存进行离线训练，避免在线rollout；并系统扫描九种压缩比，对比纯监督恢复与隐藏状态蒸馏的效果。

Result: 约8 GPU小时即可将63%压缩的学生恢复至距教师3.5个点以内。45%以内压缩时两种方法差异不显著；63%–87%区间隐藏状态蒸馏提升2.1–4.5点；CogACT在63%以上提升9.4–22.1点。81%压缩时三倍预算可将蒸馏学生与教师差距缩至平均3.9点，而监督恢复仍低20点以上。6-DoF机械臂上，72%压缩的蒸馏学生成功率77.5%（监督仅59.5%），速度比教师快2.23倍，内存少62%。

Conclusion: 完全离线的宽度剪枝加隐藏状态蒸馏可高效恢复高压缩VLA模型性能，显著优于监督恢复，并在真实机器人上兼顾成功率、延迟与内存优势；相同压缩下宽度剪枝成功率更高、深度剪枝延迟更低。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models let robots follow language instructions, but their language backbones of several billion parameters are the main obstacle to running them on robot hardware. Structured pruning reduces that backbone, and removing 63% of it from OpenVLA-OFT drops LIBERO-Long success from 93.2% to 0.8%. A recent approach restores such a model with supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning, which needs online rollouts and hundreds of GPU-hours. We recover most of the lost success entirely offline. Width pruning narrows the blocks but keeps the residual stream at its original size, so teacher and student hidden states have the same shape and are matched directly, without a projector. Training against a cache built in one teacher pass lifts the 63%-reduced student to within 3.5 points of the teacher in about 8 GPU-hours. A sweep over nine ratios locates where the recovery objective starts to matter. Up to 45% reduction the two do not differ significantly on OpenVLA-OFT. Hidden-state distillation then adds +2.1 to +4.5 points there between 63% and 87%, and +9.4 to +22.1 points on CogACT from 63% onward. At 81% on CogACT, a tripled recovery budget narrows the distilled student's gap to the teacher to 3.9 points on average, while supervised recovery stays more than 20 points below. At matched compression, width pruning yields higher success and depth pruning lower latency. On a 6-DoF manipulator, the distilled student at 72% reduction reaches 77.5% success against 59.5% for supervised recovery, runs 2.23x faster on-board than the teacher, and uses 62% less memory.

</details>


### [135] [OmniCalib: Target-Free, Task-Structured Self-Calibration for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19582)
*Kaixiang Lu,Haiyu Lan,Chunxiao Qiao,You Li,Enyu Li,Yehao Lu,Jiarui Yang,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniCalib是一种无需标定靶标、仅依赖机器人自身运动与板载传感的全身标定方法，可同时标定上肢14个关节零位、腕部与胸部相机外参及下肢、头部多相机系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装配、磨损和部件更换会使类人机器人CAD模型中编码的传感器外参和关节零位发生偏移，而现有标定流程通常只能标定单个传感器对或依赖外部基准物。

Method: 采用机器人原生任务与参数块匹配的模块化流程，检查可观性并仅将受支持的修正写入CAD模型；核心为深度ICP方法，另用四个静态双支撑姿态恢复下肢关节零位，头部模块结合多相机视觉里程计、腿部里程计与动态补偿。

Result: 相对CAD，左腕外参修正为10.56 mm和1.74度，右腕为6.33 mm和1.25度，胸部RGB-D相机为9.81 mm和0.929度，ICP点到面残差2.09 mm；14个关节零位恢复误差低于0.1度编码器分辨率；12个下肢关节零位RMS误差0.063度；头部平均SO(3)误差1.061度，最佳0.775度，优于iKalibr的0.902度。

Conclusion: 注入恢复与留出测试验证了各可观性模块的有效性，表明该无靶标全身标定流程精度高、可重复性好，可胜任类人机器人整机标定。

Abstract: Assembly, wear, and component replacement perturb the sensor extrinsics and joint zeros encoded by a humanoid CAD model. Existing procedures calibrate one sensor pair or require external fiducials. Using only robot-native motion and onboard sensing, we present OmniCalib, a target-free workflow that calibrates the full upper limbs---all 14 arm joint zeros and the extrinsics of both wrist and chest cameras---as well as lower limbs and the multi-camera head rig. Each module matches a robot-native task to a parameter block, checks observability, and writes only supported corrections to the CAD model. Our depth ICP method recovers all 14 arm joint zeros and calibrates all RGB-D camera extrinsics without any calibration target. Relative to CAD, the estimated extrinsic corrections are 10.56 mm and 1.74 degrees for the left wrist, 6.33 mm and 1.25 degrees for the right wrist, and 9.81 mm and 0.929 degrees for the chest RGB-D camera. ICP point-to-plane residual is 2.09 mm. On the same injected offsets, ICP and ArUco recover all 14 joint zeros below the 0.1-degree encoder-resolution reference. On an AGIBOT A3 Ultra humanoid, four static double-support stances recover all 12 lower-limb joint-zero offsets injected with an RMS error of 0.063 degrees. The head module combines multi-camera visual odometry with legged odometry and dynamic compensation through the live ROS transform tree. Using only planar walking, it attains a mean SO(3) error of 1.061 degrees across three sequences. The best sequence reaches 0.775 degrees, competitive with iKalibr at 0.902 degrees from rich 6-DOF excitation. Rig-relative angles repeat within 0.140 degrees. Injection recovery and held-out tests validate each observable block.

</details>


### [136] [Quantifying Mechanical Intelligence in Legged Robots with Information Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19588)
*Zach J. Patterson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文以现代腿式机器人为基准，提出信息论指标来量化“机械智能”，将机体动力学视为计算过程与通信信道，分析柔顺驱动与低减速比本体感受传动之间的权衡及其与运动控制策略的交互，并在线性腿传动模型、非线性单腿仿真和受学习策略控制的四足机器人仿真上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械智能（机器人物理形态降低控制负担的能力）已成为机器人学重要概念，在仿生、软体、集群机器人等领域均有体现，但理论理解与定量度量滞后于工程实践，亟需严谨的量化框架。

Method: 将机体动力学同时建模为计算过程与通信信道，基于信息论提出若干量化机械智能的指标；以腿式机器人为范例，量化机械模态及跨机器人坐标所处理的比特数；比较通过串联弹性驱动显式引入柔顺与采用本体感受式低减速比传动两种机制的权衡，并考察其与运动控制策略的交互；依次在简化的线性腿传动模型、非线性单腿仿真以及受学习策略控制、跨越复杂地形的四足机器人仿真上展开。

Result: 证明若干既有腿式机器人工程经验可用信息论描述，并量化了机械模态与跨坐标的信息处理过程；揭示了串联弹性驱动与低减速比本体感受传动在与控制策略配合时的权衡关系；结果在从线性到非线性仿真、再到学习策略控制的四足机器人等复杂度递增的系统中得到体现。

Conclusion: 该工作为更广泛地研究机器人机构及其在具身计算中的作用奠定了基础，推动机械智能从工程直觉走向可量化的理论框架。

Abstract: Mechanical intelligence, loosely defined as the reduction in control burden afforded by a robot's physical form, has become a prominent concept in robotics, with instantiations in bioinspired robotics, soft robotics, robotic swarms, and many other areas. However, rigorous theoretical understanding and quantitative measures of mechanical intelligence have lagged behind the engineering systems that the community has developed. In this work, using modern legged robots as a benchmark and exemplar, we propose several information-theoretic metrics for quantifying mechanical intelligence. By viewing body dynamics as both a computational process and a communication channel, we show that several prior insights in legged-robot engineering can be described using information theory, and we quantify how bits are processed by mechanical modes and across robot coordinates. Specifically, we examine the trade-off between explicitly incorporating compliance through series-elastic actuation and using so-called proprioceptive, low-gear-ratio transmissions, and we explore how these mechanisms interact with control policies during locomotion. We develop these results on systems of increasing complexity: a simplified linear model of a robot-leg transmission, a nonlinear single-leg simulation, and simulated quadruped robots controlled by a learned policy while navigating challenging terrain. These results lay the groundwork for broader study of robot mechanisms and their role in embodied computation.

</details>


### [137] [WorldContact: A Contact-Centric World Model for Scalable Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19600)
*Caoliwen Wang,Mengdi Wang,Heng Zhang,Shixun Huang,Siyuan Chen,Chao Liu,Anpei Chen,Zhendong Wang,Peter Yichen Chen,Huamin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WorldContact——一种以接触为中心的可变形物体操作世界模型，能用少量高质量轨迹高效生成额外训练数据，加速机器人策略适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人适应新物体和新任务需要交互经验，但获取这些经验成本高昂；源数值模拟器因需小积分步长以避免穿插，运行效率低。

Method: 构建以接触为中心的世界模型，用比源模拟器更大的时间步长预测物体动力学，从有限的高质量轨迹中生成额外训练数据，并用于微调视觉-语言-动作策略。

Result: 在16个购物袋操作任务上评测；单张H100 GPU上状态推演比源模拟器快10倍（不含渲染与磁盘I/O）；提袋任务中，仅用源仿真数据微调成功率为65%，用WorldContact扩展数据后提升至95%。

Conclusion: WorldContact支持高效数据生成，可有效用于机器人策略适配。

Abstract: Adapting robots to new objects and tasks requires interaction experience that can be costly to obtain. We present WorldContact, a contact-centric world model for deformable-object manipulation, constructed from a limited set of high-quality trajectories to generate additional training data efficiently. It predicts object dynamics using larger time steps than the source numerical simulator, which requires small integration steps to resolve rapid motion and prevent interpenetration. We evaluate WorldContact across 16 shopping-bag manipulation tasks. State-rollout measurements on a single H100 GPU show a $10\times$ speedup over the source simulator, excluding rendering and disk I/O. We use the generated data to fine-tune an existing vision-language-action policy and deploy it directly on a real robot. In bag lifting, the same policy achieves 65% single-attempt success when fine-tuned on source simulation data alone, compared with 95% when fine-tuned on the dataset expanded with WorldContact. These results support efficient data generation with WorldContact for robot policy adaptation.

</details>


### [138] [TacSushi: Tactile-Grounded World-Action Modeling for Dexterous Sushi Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19613)
*Haodi Hu,Kaen Kogashi,Toshiaki Koike-Akino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacSushi 是一种基于触觉与 Cosmos3 的世界-动作策略，通过训练期未来后果预测监督与特征级门控触觉融合，提升食物操作的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧食物操作面临形变、遮挡和接触不确定性等挑战，需要有效的触觉信息融合与对后果的建模能力。

Method: 骨干网络编码当前 RGB、语言和手部状态；采用特征级门控融合将指尖触觉特征并入动作表示。训练时用基于示范动作块的条件解码器预测未来视觉观测、任务进度、相对接触风险与触觉摘要，部署时移除该解码器；失败试验仅提供后果监督，其动作不参与模仿学习。

Result: 在 340 次成功与 50 次失败真实机器人试验上训练，600 次独立 rollout（三个分布内任务、两个分布外成分配料变体）中，完整 TacSushi 分布内成功率 68.3%、分布外 37.5%；去除未来后果监督为 36.7%/10.0%；以直接触觉拼接替代门控融合为 25.0%/17.5%。终局结果用锚定视觉质量协议评分，均衡融合五项人工评分与三项视觉语言模型评分。

Conclusion: 结果支持特征级门控触觉融合与仅训练期预测监督具有互补收益。

Abstract: Dexterous food manipulation requires control under deformation, occlusion, and uncertain contact. We present TacSushi, a tactile-grounded, Cosmos3-based world-action policy that learns from recorded future consequences while acting on current observations. The backbone encodes current RGB, language, and hand state, and feature-wise gated fusion incorporates fingertip tactile features into the action representation. During training, a decoder conditioned on demonstrated action chunks predicts logged future visual observations, task progress, relative contact risk, and tactile summaries; this decoder is removed at deployment. Failed trials provide consequence supervision, but their actions are excluded from imitation. We train TacSushi on 340 successful and 50 failed real-robot trials and compare six methods in 600 separate rollouts across three in-distribution tasks and two out-of-distribution ingredient variants. To assess food quality beyond a single geometric threshold, we score terminal outcomes using an anchored visual-quality protocol that equally weights five human ratings and three vision-language-model ratings per rollout. Full TacSushi achieves 68.3% average in-distribution success and 37.5% out-of-distribution success, compared with 36.7%/10.0% without future-consequence supervision and 25.0%/17.5% with direct tactile concatenation in place of gated fusion. These comparisons support complementary benefits of feature-wise gated tactile fusion and training-only predictive supervision.

</details>


### [139] [EmbodiedMind: Adaptive Data Curation and Prefix-Tree Reinforcement Learning for Efficient Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19659)
*Feifan Wang,Zongbing Zhang,Yu Zhang,Lingfeng Wang,Yurui Zhu,Jin Deng,Mingliang Zhang,Zhengguang Gao,Yongcheng Wang,Jin Xu,Ri Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效具身基础模型训练范式EmbodiedMind，通过RSFT、IR-GRPO与Trie-GRPO三阶段协同，在18个基准上取得70.02%的平均性能，显著提升长程任务规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身基础模型训练依赖大规模数据和算力，但存在三大瓶颈：低信息样本导致样本利用率低、异构任务间梯度贡献不均衡、以及长程规划中轨迹级奖励对所有token无差别惩罚带来的严重信用分配问题。因此需要一种更高效的训练范式。

Method: 方法包含三个协同阶段：1）RSFT（基于拒绝采样的微调）过滤低信息样本，建立稳健行为先验并防止分布坍缩；2）IR-GRPO（迭代拒绝GRPO）使用按难度分层的任务特定队列，在强化学习迭代中保持数据均衡，并配合混合奖励机制实现精确跨任务反馈；3）Trie-GRPO，一种基于动作前缀树的强化学习算法，实现步级优势估计，通过隔离中间正确决策与下游错误解决信用分配问题，并平衡探索效率与深度。

Result: EmbodiedMind在18个基准上取得70.02%的最先进平均性能，在长程任务规划准确率上显著优于其他具身基础模型。

Conclusion: 该三阶段训练范式有效缓解样本利用率低、梯度不均衡和信用分配难题，实现了具身基础模型的性能领先，项目将开源以支持可复现性。

Abstract: Training embodied foundation models typically requires massive-scale datasets and extensive computational resources, yet often suffers from three critical limitations: (1) inefficient sample utilization due to low-informative samples; (2) imbalanced gradient contributions across heterogeneous tasks; and (3) severe credit assignment problem in long-horizon planning, where trajectory-level rewards indiscriminately penalize all tokens. To address these issues, we propose an efficient training paradigm that achieves state-of-the-art average performance through strategic data selection and hierarchical policy optimization. Our approach consists of three synergistic stages. First, Rejection Sampling-based Fine-Tuning (RSFT) filters out low-informative samples to establish robust behavioral priors while preventing distributional collapse. Second, Iterative Rejection GRPO (IR-GRPO) employs task-specific queues stratified by difficulty to keep datasets balanced across reinforcement learning iterations, coupled with a hybrid reward mechanism for precise cross-task feedback. Third, to enhance long-horizon task planning, we introduce Trie-GRPO, a novel reinforcement learning algorithm based on action prefix trees, which enables step-level advantage estimation.
