For control, the framework decouples yaw regulation from translational control, avoiding computationally expensive coupled multi-DOF optimization and enabling real-time translational MPC. The translational controller employs adaptive model-fusion predictive control, combining constant-velocity and zero-velocity target models to accommodate different target-motion patterns. It updates the model weights using historical prediction errors and computes translational commands subject to actuation, following-distance, and field-of-view constraints. Through simulations and real-world experiments, we validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework and show it has better performance than existing frameworks.

</details>


### [211] [MILER: Semantic Mid-Level Representation for Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning in Unstructured Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20747)
*Thomas Steinecker,Denis Trescher,Alexander Bienemann,Thorsten Luettel,Mirko Maehlisch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MILER是一种端到端策略框架，通过语义中层表征仿真器与轨迹对齐策略，实现了自动驾驶在非结构化环境中的零样本仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在自动驾驶中潜力巨大，但在非结构化环境中的实际应用稀少，主要受制于仿真到现实迁移的困难。

Method: 离线训练阶段使用自定义的语义中层表征（MLR）仿真器，用强化学习训练策略网络，控制输出直接作用于自行车模型；实车部署时用BEVFusion处理相机与LiDAR数据，生成与MLR仿真器一致的语义鸟瞰图表征，并通过轨迹对齐策略实现感知与控制的零样本迁移。

Result: 在包含多种障碍物、发夹弯、最高33.6 km/h速度及越野路段的3.0 km多样化测试赛道上，用两辆不同车辆无人干预行驶17.3 km，验证了方法有效性；整套软件栈运行于Jetson AGX Orin。

Conclusion: MILER证明了借助语义中层表征与轨迹对齐，可在非结构化环境中实现零样本仿真到现实的端到端自动驾驶。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning constitutes a promising approach owing to its potential for superhuman performance and self-learned policies. However, its application to real-world autonomous driving remains scarce, particularly in unstructured environments, because of the challenges associated with sim-to-real transfer for unstructured environments. In this work, we present MILER, an end-to-end policy framework with zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. During offline training, we employ a custom semantic mid-level representation (MLR) simulator and train the policy network using reinforcement learning, with its control outputs applied directly to a bicycle model. During deployment on the real vehicle, camera and LiDAR data are processed by BEVFusion to generate a semantic bird's-eye-view representation consistent with that of the MLR simulator. The actions generated by the policy network are not applied directly to the real vehicle. Instead, we employ a trajectory-alignment strategy that enables zero-shot sim-to-real transfer of both perception and control. We extensively evaluate the proposed framework on a diverse test track comprising numerous challenges, including various obstacles, hairpin curves, velocities of up to 33.6 km/h, and off-road sections. In total, we drove 17.3 km with two different vehicles on a 3.0 km test track without human intervention, thereby demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach. Furthermore, the entire software stack runs on a Jetson AGX Orin.

</details>


### [212] [OPTED: On-Policy Fine-Tuning for End-to-End Driving using a Render-Free Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20756)
*Damiano Da Col,Maximilian Igl,Peter Karkus,Kashyap Chitta,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Konrad Schindler,Christos Sakaridis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OPTED方法，将强化学习从端到端驾驶策略的后训练中解耦：先用RL在向量化输入（高精地图和边界框）上训练特权教师模型，再由该教师在闭环后训练中监督预训练的相机学生模型。在AlpaSim中基于真实驾驶日志的3DGS重建对TransFuser和VaVAM微调，驾驶分数分别提升1.6倍和9.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅扩大预训练数据收益递减，后训练在自动驾驶等物理AI领域愈发重要。端到端驾驶策略通过行为克隆在人类演示上开环预训练，但闭环部署时的误差累积会使车辆偏离训练分布，增加安全关键事故风险。闭环后训练可缓解该风险，但基于传感器的策略需要昂贵的仿真，直接RL后训练成本高。

Method: OPTED将强化学习与端到端策略后训练解耦：首先利用RL在向量化输入（高精地图与边界框）上训练一个特权教师模型；随后在闭环后训练阶段，由该教师为预训练的学生模型提供监督。方法应用于两个相机模型TransFuser和VaVAM，并在AlpaSim中利用真实驾驶日志的神经重建（3DGS）进行微调。

Result: TransFuser和VaVAM的驾驶分数分别提升1.6倍和9.5倍。在受控实验中，OPTED在达到与直接RL后训练相当的闭环性能的同时，所需的仿真交互次数减少约三个数量级，且更接近人类先验。

Conclusion: OPTED通过特权教师监督的方式实现了高效的闭环端到端驾驶后训练，在大幅减少仿真交互成本的同时显著提升性能并保持对人类行为的贴近性，为传感器端到端策略的闭环后训练提供了实用方案。

Abstract: As scaling pre-training data alone yields diminishing returns, post-training is becoming increasingly important across physical AI domains such as autonomous driving. End-to-end driving policies are pre-trained in open loop with behavior cloning on human demonstrations. However, compounding errors during closed-loop deployment can take the vehicle outside the training data distribution, increasing the risk of safety-critical incidents. Closed-loop post-training can mitigate this risk but requires costly simulation for sensor-based policies. We propose OPTED (on-policy fine-tuning for end-to-end driving) which decouples reinforcement learning from the post-training of the end-to-end policy: a privileged teacher is trained using RL on vectorized inputs (HD-map and bounding boxes). This teacher then provides supervision to the pre-trained student during closed-loop post-training. We apply OPTED to two camera-based models, TransFuser and VaVAM, and fine-tune them in AlpaSim, using neural reconstructions (3DGS) of real driving logs. Driving scores increase by factors of 1.6$\times$ and 9.5$\times$, respectively. In controlled experiments OPTED matches closed-loop performance with approximately three orders of magnitude fewer simulator interactions than direct RL post-training, while staying closer to the human prior. Project page: https://01dami23.github.io/opted/

</details>


### [213] [Agile-WAM: An Agile Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20761)
*Hanchu Zhou,Brendan Lynch,Raman Goyal,Dechen Gao,Begum Kasap,Boqi Zhao,Junshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种敏捷的触觉世界行动模型，用于富接触机器人控制，兼顾多模态协同预测与低延迟推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉世界行动模型依赖大规模预训练生成骨干来建模富接触物理动态，导致推理效率低、部署不灵活，难以满足高频精确控制需求。

Method: 将视觉与触觉观测编码到共享隐空间，作为视觉-触觉到动作的流匹配过程的源，联合生成动作块隐表示与未来视觉/触觉隐表示；并引入多时域多模态预测，视觉隐表示采用更大时间偏移监督，触觉隐表示预测下一帧以捕捉细粒度接触动态。

Result: 在九个仿真和五个真实富接触操作任务中表现强且鲁棒，成功率超过最强基线；五个真实实验中整体成功率相对提升29.4%，推理延迟11.9毫秒。

Conclusion: 多模态世界行动模型可以以敏捷架构实现，适用于精确且高频的机器人控制。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) advance beyond conventional visuomotor policies by jointly predicting future world states and robot actions, enabling the policy to learn physical dynamics that support effective control. However, recent tactile WAMs often rely on large-scale pretrained generative backbones to capture contact-rich physical dynamics, which limit their inference efficiency and flexible deployment. In this paper, we present \ABBR{}, an agile tactile World Action Model for contact-rich robot control. \ABBR{} encodes visual and tactile observations into a shared latent that serves as the source of a direct vision-tactile-to-action flow-matching process, which can jointly generate latent representations of action chunks and future visual/tactile latents. A key observation is that vision and tactile signals evolve at inherently different timescales: adjacent visual frames are often highly similar, whereas tactile signals can change abruptly upon contact. We therefore introduce multi-horizon multimodal prediction in \ABBR{}, which provides supervision for visual latent at a larger temporal offset while predicting the tactile latent in the next frame to capture fine-grained contact dynamics. Across nine simulated and five real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, \ABBR{} demonstrates strong and robust performance, outperforming the strongest baseline in success rate while maintaining low inference latency. In particular, in five real-world experiments, \ABBR{} yields a relative gain of $\textbf{29.4\%}$ in overall success rates while achieving inference latency of $\textbf{11.9 ms}$. These results demonstrate that multimodal WAM can be achieved with an agile architecture suitable for precise and high-frequency robot control. More details are available on our project page: https://hanchuzhou.github.io/TARO_project_page/.

</details>


### [214] [Coding Agents with an Obstacle-Aware Harness for Safe Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20822)
*Bingxin Xu,Yuzhang Shang,Zhen Dong,Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 编码代理在机器人操作中缺乏安全性，论文提出 SafeHarness 通过障碍感知的路线规划与接触执行两个模块，使安全约束成为优先项，将任务成功率提升至71.9%、避碰率提升至87.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理已被证明可在无需机器人专门训练的情况下操作机器人，但其安全性从未被考察。论文在"任务目标+不可触碰障碍"的安全约束下评估该范式，发现代理在大多数情况下会撞上障碍，将任务完成视为唯一目标而忽视安全。由于代理的推理轨迹中已提及障碍、提示中也已禁止触碰，故问题不在感知或指令，而在于规划——安全约束从未成为优先项。

Method: 论文将操作分解为"路径阶段"与"接触密集时刻"以定位失败根源：路径阶段模型无法优先考虑安全约束，缺乏"畅通路线"概念，也缺乏路线不可行时的重规划能力；接触时刻模型未意识到接触执行同样受该约束限制。为此提出 SafeHarness，赋予模型两个障碍感知工具：障碍感知路线规划将物体表示为边界框并绘制由路点组成的候选路线，代理预先规划路线、验证、必要时重规划后再执行；障碍感知接触执行则选择使接触本身避开障碍的接触位置。

Result: SafeHarness 达到71.9%的任务成功率和87.5%的避碰率，分别超越此前 SOTA 6.5%和27.0%；这两项结果分别是同一代理在无该工具时的2.3倍和1.5倍。

Conclusion: 编码代理范式在机器人操作中默认忽视安全约束，且失败源于规划而非感知或指令。通过在规划与接触两个层面引入障碍感知的显式机制，可让模型真正将安全约束置于优先地位，从而在保持任务能力的同时显著提升安全性。

Abstract: Coding agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot manipulation: a language model writes the robot controller as a program, and agents built in this way now operate robots without robot-specific training.Whether this paradigm is also safe, however, has not been asked. We evaluate coding agent under a safety constraint, where each task pairs a manipulation goal with an obstacle the robot must not touch. The agent pursues the goal but collides with the obstacle in most cases, treating task completion as its sole objective while neglecting safety. The agent reasons about the obstacle in its traces, and the prompt already forbids touching it, so neither perception nor instruction is at fault; the fault lies in the planning, where the stated constraint never becomes a priority. By decomposing manipulation into a route phase and a contact-rich moment, we locate the source of the failure. Along the route, the model cannot prioritize the safety constraint, having no notion of a clearing route and none of replanning once a chosen route becomes infeasible. At the contact, it is unaware that contact execution is bounded by the same constraint. To close this gap, we present SafeHarness, which equips the model with two obstacle-aware harnesses that enable it to prioritize the safety constraint. Obstacle-aware route planning grounds the objects as bounding boxes and draws candidate routes over them as sequences of waypoints. The agent then plans a route in advance, verifies it, replans when necessary, and only then executes it. Obstacle-aware contact execution instead selects the contact position so that the contact itself avoids the obstacle. SafeHarness attains 71.9% task success and 87.5% collision avoidance, surpassing the previous SOTA by 6.5% and 27.0%, respectively. These results are $2.3\times$ and $1.5\times$ those of the same agent without harnesses.

</details>


### [215] [GeoAAC: Geometry-Based Adaptive Action Chunking from Denoising Trajectories in VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20776)
*Xin Chen,Sen Chen,Yujuan Ding,Jian Liu,Guoqing Wang,Wei Ye,Heng Tao Shen,Yi Bin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GeoAAC，基于几何信息的自适应动作分块方法，用于流式VLA策略，根据预测可靠性动态调整动作时域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略常用固定动作时域，但任务不同阶段对动作连续性、控制精度和闭环反馈的需求不同，固定时域难以适应变化的控制需求。

Method: GeoAAC利用Flow Matching去噪轨迹的几何信息表征预测可靠性（动作前缀的几何变化与预测不确定性正相关），基于前缀几何构建时域几何剖面，在单次生成中自适应确定动作时域，无需额外训练。

Result: 在GR00T N1.5和π0.5上，于LIBERO、LIBERO-Pro、RoboCasa365及真实操作任务中，相比固定时域基线和现有自适应方法均有一致提升，仿真最高提升8.7个百分点，真实世界平均成功率从53.3%提升至74.4%。

Conclusion: 几何信息可为流式VLA策略提供有效的预测可靠性过程级信号，实现无需训练的自适应动作分块，显著优于固定时域与现有自适应方法。

Abstract: Action chunking is widely used for action generation and execution in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies, yet existing approaches commonly use a fixed action horizon. During a rollout, different task stages may require different levels of action continuity, control precision, and closed-loop feedback, making a fixed horizon unable to accommodate changing control requirements. We propose \textbf{GeoAAC}, a geometry-based adaptive action chunking method for flow-based VLA policies that adjusts the action horizon according to the reliability of the current action prediction. We show that the geometry of Flow Matching denoising trajectories provides process-level information for characterizing prediction reliability, with geometric variation across action prefixes remaining positively correlated with predictive uncertainty. GeoAAC uses this prefix-wise geometry to construct a horizon-wise geometric profile and adaptively determine the action horizon from a single generation without additional training. Experiments with GR00T N1.5 and π0.5 on LIBERO, LIBERO-Pro, RoboCasa365, and real-world manipulation tasks show consistent improvements over fixed-action-horizon baselines and existing adaptive methods, including up to 8.7 percentage points in simulation and an increase in average real-world success rate from 53.3\% to 74.4\%.

</details>


### [216] [StageGuard: Learning Stage Transitions for Long-Horizon Robot Tasks via Agentic Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20791)
*Jinbang Huang,Yuanzhao Hu,Zhiyuan Li,Ran Qi,Yixin Xiao,Yangzheng Wu,Tengyue Ba,Zhanguang Zhang,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出StageGuard，一种agentic蒸馏框架，实现准确高效的分阶段转换决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级规划框架组合多个机器人控制策略执行长时程任务，关键在于判断何时终止当前技能并进入下一子任务，但现有方法依赖难以获取的预设计完成信号检查器；大模型VLM虽推理强，但决策边界与任务完成标准不对齐，且云端部署与冗长推理带来高延迟，限制实时监控。

Method: StageGuard结合教师模型推理与演示轨迹，生成子任务完成与策略切换的结构化解释；轻量级学生VLM利用这些解释生成紧凑的自解释，并进行监督微调。

Result: 在两个基准的轨迹上评估分阶段转换预测，并通过集成到BEHAVIOR-1K的层级机器人控制评估闭环任务成功率，并在真实机器人上进一步验证；结果显示分阶段转换预测显著提升，同时支持高效在线监控。

Conclusion: StageGuard通过agentic蒸馏实现准确高效的分阶段转换决策，兼顾预测性能与在线实时监控需求。

Abstract: Hierarchical planning frameworks combine skills from multiple robot control policies for long-horizon task execution, where determining when to terminate the current skill and advance to the next subtask is essential. Existing approaches often rely on pre-designed completion signal checkers that are hard to obtain in real-world execution. Large-scale vision-language models (VLMs) offer strong reasoning capabilities, but their decision boundaries are not inherently aligned with task completion criteria, while cloud deployment and lengthy reasoning introduce substantial latency, limiting real-time monitoring. We propose StageGuard, an agentic distillation framework for accurate and efficient stage-transition decisions. StageGuard combines teacher-model reasoning with demonstration trajectories to generate structured explanations of subtask completion and policy switching. A lightweight student VLM uses these explanations to generate compact self-explanations, which are used for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate stage-transition prediction on trajectories from two benchmarks and assess closed-loop task success through integration into hierarchical robot control on BEHAVIOR-1K, with further validation on real robots. Results show substantial improvements in stage-transition prediction while supporting efficient online monitoring.

</details>


### [217] [Workspace Models: Lightweight Robotic Memory via Saliency-Driven Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20820)
*Nitish Dashora,Douglas Chen,Idan Shenfeld,John Marangola,Pulkit Agrawal,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"workspace token"，通过在训练时将耗时的VLM查询蒸馏为一个轻量级潜在记忆表示，部署时可高效查询并替代原始观测，无需在线VLM推理，在仿真和真实硬件上均能解决记忆密集型机器人操作任务，且性能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂机器人操作任务需要长期记忆历史事件与动作。直接以完整历史作为条件会导致策略产生虚假相关并降低性能；而现有基于策略记忆的方法依赖在线循环调用昂贵的VLM查询来提取任务相关信息，计算成本高。

Method: 提出workspace token表示，训练分两步：(1) 用VLM识别完成任务所需的当前与历史信息；(2) 通过集合重建解码器损失将这些信息蒸馏进workspace token。该token作为轻量潜在记忆，训练时集中进行VLM查询，部署时高效查询。

Result: 在仿真和真实硬件上，workspace token可作为观测的即插即用替代，使策略无需在线VLM推理即可解决记忆密集型任务。且workspace token不仅更轻量，还能带来更好的策略性能。

Conclusion: 将密集VLM查询前移到训练阶段、蒸馏为轻量潜在记忆，是解决机器人策略长期记忆问题的有效替代方案，兼顾效率与性能。

Abstract: Complex robotic manipulation tasks frequently require a long-term memory of past events and actions. As conditioning on full histories renders policies prone to spurious correlations and degrades performance, many approaches to policy memory involve compressing historical information through expensive VLM queries in-the-loop to process only task-salient information. In this paper, we propose an alternative approach in which computationally intensive VLM queries are made during train-time to learn a lightweight latent memory that can be efficiently queried at deployment time. Our representation, which we call the \textbf{workspace token}, is trained by (1) using a VLM to identify current and historical information necessary for completing a task, then (2) distilling these into the workspace token using a set-reconstruction decoder loss. In both simulation and hardware, we show that the workspace token can be used as a drop-in replacement for observations during deployment, enabling policies to solve memory-intensive tasks without the need for VLM reasoning in-the-loop. Interestingly, we found that workspace tokens are not only more lightweight but also lead to better policy performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning: Stability under Constant Stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19170)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Skylar Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过构造双状态反例揭示ETD采样动力学的非稳定性，并提出正则化强调式时序差分学习（RETD）以修复冲击后动力学，证明了几乎必然收敛与条件矩收缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ETD能稳定期望off-policy TD更新并改变投影几何，但这两者都不能决定常步长下的采样动力学。作者希望厘清ETD均值映射收缩与采样乘积Lyapunov指数符号之间的分离现象，并加以修正。

Method: 构造遍历性双状态反例，使ETD均值映射收缩而采样乘积具有正的上Lyapunov指数；用再生周期分析将该符号与follow-on trace的无穷方差分离；提出RETD——一种归一化的一阶冲击后修复方法，保持trace与重要性比率不变，将强调信号存入泄漏标量状态并释放延迟修正。

Result: RETD的原始均衡是ETD均衡的仿射平移，单/双正则化读出可精确恢复ETD不动点；在调和递减步长下证明几乎必然收敛，并基于马尔可夫随机乘积界给出条件常步长矩收缩结果。RETD在双状态构造和一个Baird点上具有可认证的负指数，而Baird的ETD正号仍为数值结果。配对10,000次实验验证了两种分离、不动点恢复、非单调稳定域及任务依赖性。

Conclusion: RETD改变了冲击后动力学，但不降低共享的follow-on-trace方差；其为ETD采样不稳定提供了理论诊断与可证明的修复方案。

Abstract: Emphatic temporal-difference learning (ETD) stabilizes the expected off-policy TD update and changes its projection geometry, but neither property determines constant-stepsize sampled dynamics. We construct an ergodic two-state counterexample in which the ETD mean map contracts while the sampled product has a positive top Lyapunov exponent. Regenerative-cycle analysis separates this sign from the infinite variance of the follow-on trace. We introduce regularized emphatic TD (RETD), a normalized first-order post-shock repair that leaves the trace and importance ratios unchanged, stores the emphatic TD signal in a leaky scalar state, and releases a delayed correction. RETD's raw equilibrium is an affine shift of the ETD equilibrium; single- and two-regularization readouts recover the ETD fixed point exactly. We prove almost-sure convergence for harmonic diminishing stepsizes and a conditional constant-stepsize moment-contraction result from a Markovian random-product bound. RETD has certified negative exponents on the two-state construction and one Baird point, whereas the positive Baird ETD sign remains numerical. Paired 10,000-run experiments validate both separations, fixed-point recovery, a nonmonotone stability region, and task dependence. RETD changes post-shock dynamics; it does not reduce the shared follow-on-trace variance.

</details>


### [219] [BioPhys-Bridge: A Benchmark for Interdisciplinary Scientific Reasoning in Physics-Grounded Biological Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19180)
*Qingyang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BioPhys-Bridge，一个面向生物物理文献证据化科学推理的基准数据集，包含500个案例、1517个agent任务，覆盖6个生物学领域与9类物理模型，并提供证据ID、定量数值、方程与机制等接地目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在跨学科科研文献分析中面临独特挑战：生物物理研究中的可靠回答需要将观测数据锚定于原文证据，通过定量物理模型加以解释，并关联到生物机制。现有基准难以覆盖这种多步、跨学科的证据化推理需求。

Method: 构建BioPhys-Bridge基准，每个案例包含证据块、稳定证据ID、定量数值、单位、方程、假设、机制与后续决策作为QA和RAG的接地目标；通过schema、证据完整性、定量接地、来源许可、去重、单位归一化等严格质量门控，并对81个案例进行领域专家评审与标注。

Result: 初步评测显示DeepSeek-V4-Flash取得最高证据ID F1（0.360），其次为Qwen3.7-Max（0.316）和GPT-4o-mini（0.294）。

Conclusion: BioPhys-Bridge是用于评估归因、忠实性、幻觉抑制及生物实验设计的跨学科基准，支持复杂多步科学推理；未来将扩大数据规模与复杂度并开展全面评测，代码与数据已在GitHub与Hugging Face公开。

Abstract: Language models face unique challenges in analyzing interdisciplinary scientific research literature. In biophysics research, faithful answers require grounding observed data in source evidence, interpreting it through a quantitative physics model, and linking it to a biological mechanism. To address this challenge, we introduce BioPhys-Bridge, a novel benchmark dataset for evidence-grounded scientific reasoning over biophysical literature. Each case contains evidence blocks, stable evidence IDs, quantitative values, units, equations, assumptions, mechanisms, and next decisions as grounding targets for question answering (QA) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The initial release contains 500 cases, 1,517 agent-facing tasks, and covers six biological domains and nine physical model families, including three sparse families reserved for future expansion. We enforce strict quality gates for all cases in schema, evidence-integrity, quantitative-grounding, source-license, duplicate, unit-normalization, with domain expert review and annotation for 81 cases. Preliminary evaluations show that DeepSeek-V4-Flash obtain the highest evidence-ID $F_1$ score (0.360), followed by Qwen3.7-Max (0.316) and GPT-4o-mini (0.294). BioPhys-Bridge is an interdisciplinary benchmark for evaluating attribution, faithfulness, hallucination reduction, and biological experiment design with complex, multi-step scientific reasoning. Future works will increase the size and complexity of the dataset and perform comprehensive evaluations. Code and data are available in the GitHub repository and on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [220] [What Do We Expect from LLMs? Mapping the Design of LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19182)
*Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统梳理了2022年1月至2026年8月arXiv上14,767篇引入或更新评测资源的论文，通过分层筛选与自动全文编码，刻画了LLM评测在目标系统与领域、评测材料与条件、评分机制三方面的变化趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排行榜只能反映模型优劣，却难以揭示评测需求本身的演变。作者希望从不断扩充的评测基准中，反推研究者对LLM的能力期待及成功标准，理解公共研究如何将能力预期转化为具体测试与判据。

Method: 对2022年1月至2026年8月的arXiv投稿进行系统性图谱绘制，共纳入14,767篇引入或更新评测资源的论文；采用分层筛选（staged screening）与自动化全文编码，分析目标系统与领域、评测材料与条件、评分机制的变化。

Result: 评测日益强调行动、交互与专业应用，新旧设计元素常并存；模型参与度发展不均衡：LLM评分在智能体与非智能体组中均增长，而模型生成材料在近期队列中未见持续增长。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了公共研究如何将能力期待落地为具体测试与成功标准。随着AI参与构造测试、执行任务与评判回答，也引出关键疑问：评测的扩张究竟带来更多独立证据，还是可能复制参与模型的偏好与盲区？

Abstract: Benchmarks are central to how progress in large language models (LLMs) is assessed and communicated. Yet model rankings alone reveal little about how evaluation requirements themselves are changing. The expanding variety of benchmarks offers another perspective: what researchers expect LLMs to do, and what they count as successful performance. We systematically map 14,767 papers introducing or updating evaluation resources from arXiv submissions between January 2022 and August 2026. Using staged screening and automated full-text coding, we examine changes in target systems and domains, evaluation materials and conditions, and scoring mechanisms. The collection shows growing emphasis on action, interaction, and professional applications, while established and newer design elements frequently coexist. Model participation also develops unevenly: LLM-based scoring grows within both agent and non-agent groups, whereas model-generated materials show no comparable sustained increase in recent cohorts. These findings illuminate how public research translates capability expectations into concrete tests and criteria for success. As AI participates in constructing tests, performing tasks, and judging responses, they also raise a question: does expanding evaluation provide more independent evidence, or risk reproducing the preferences and blind spots of its participating models?

</details>


### [221] [Position: It is Time to Virtualize Foundation Models with a Self-evolving Operating System Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19203)
*Suparna Bhattacharya,Tarun Kumar,Cong Xu,Satish Kumar Mopur,Jiahao Li,Ashish Mishra,Aalap Tripathy,Annmary Justine Koomthanam,Martin Foltin,Ian Foster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张AI领域需要一个"基础模型操作系统"（FMOS），作为类比虚拟机抽象硬件的系统层，来虚拟化基础模型交互，为应用提供专用、可信、能力近乎无限的基础模型实例假象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI应用已从单一单体基础模型转向复合智能体系统，但当前技术栈高度碎片化：即便MCP、A2A等协议简化了工具与智能体的连接，每个框架仍内嵌隐式运行时（负责状态、记忆、预算、护栏），导致行为不可移植、治理脆弱。这种状况类似操作系统出现之前的计算时代，每个程序都要重复实现基础服务。

Method: 提出FMOS概念，作为虚拟化基础模型交互的系统层。内部负责跨记忆层级的知识编排、模型选择与资源分配、验证与策略执行。借鉴人脑在快速直觉与缓慢审慎之间切换的机制，FMOS学习何时介入、何时让推理直接进行，并基于运行经验持续调整策略。

Result: 本文为立场论文，未提供实验或量化结果，主要通过类比（操作系统、虚拟机、人脑双系统）论证FMOS的必要性与设计理念。

Conclusion: AI领域应当构建FMOS这一系统层，以解决当前智能体技术栈的碎片化与治理脆弱问题，实现基础模型交互的虚拟化、可移植性与可信性。

Abstract: AI applications have shifted from single, monolithic foundation models (FM) to compound agentic systems. Yet today's stacks remain fragmented: even as protocols (e.g., MCP, A2A) ease tool/agent connectivity, each framework embeds an implicit runtime for state, memory, budgets, and guardrails, making behavior non-portable and governance brittle. It mirrors computing before operating systems, when every program re-implemented basic services. This position paper argues that the field now needs a Foundation Model Operating System (FMOS) -- a system layer that virtualizes FM interactions analogous to how virtual machines abstract physical hardware, giving applications the illusion of dedicated, trustworthy FM instances with effectively unbounded capabilities. Internally, the FMOS orchestrates knowledge across memory tiers, model selection and resource allocation, and verification and policy enforcement. Like the human brain switching between fast intuition and slow deliberation, the FMOS learns when to intervene and when to let inference proceed directly and continuously adapting its policies based on operational experience.

</details>


### [222] [What Do Current Systematic Generalization Tasks Miss? A Reasoning-Centered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19212)
*Chengwen Qi,Deheng Ye,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出TranSGrid测试平台，将演绎、归纳与溯因推理统一到一个任务中，以揭示现有系统泛化研究因简化设定而遗漏的能力要点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统泛化（重新组合已知原子元素解决新问题）是智能核心，但现有研究依赖近似线性动作组合、基于产出性的测试、动作显式目标等简化，虽便于研究却遗漏了该能力的关键方面。作者希望刻画这些简化到底遗漏了什么。

Method: 采用以推理为中心的视角，构建TranSGrid测试平台，在统一任务中同时引入演绎、归纳和溯因三种推理。用七个Transformer模型在4,800个TranSGrid实例上做实验，并与留出测试集对比；同时向TranSGrid中重新引入原先的两种简化（一种使动作近似线性组合以降低归纳需求，另一种使目标动作显式以降低溯因需求），观察求解率变化。

Result: 所有模型在TranSGrid上表现远差于留出测试集：最大模型解决79.6%的测试集，但仅解决55.3%的TranSGrid和15.8%的最难子集；差距在训练长度范围内依然存在，说明产出性本身不足以评估系统泛化。引入任一简化后，求解率都回到接近测试集水平，表明任一种简化都足以把TranSGrid退化为普通留出测试集。

Conclusion: 现有任务至少降低了归纳或溯因需求之一；要全面衡量系统泛化，需要同时涉及演绎、归纳、溯因三种推理的任务。

Abstract: Systematic generalization, the ability to solve novel problems by recombining known atomic elements, is central to human intelligence but difficult to study rigorously under controlled settings. Existing studies therefore rely on simplifications such as approximately linear action composition, productivity-based tests, and action-explicit goals, which make systematic generalization easier to study but omit some essential aspects of this capability. To characterize what these simplifications miss, we adopt a reasoning-centered lens and introduce TranSGrid, a testbed that brings deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning together within a unified task. Experiments with seven Transformers on 4,800 TranSGrid instances show that all models perform much worse on TranSGrid than on a held-out test set: the largest model solves 79.6% of the test set, but only 55.3% of TranSGrid and 15.8% of the hardest subset. The gap remains within the training length range, showing that productivity alone is not sufficient to evaluate systematic generalization. Additionally, we reintroduce the other two simplifications into TranSGrid: one variant makes actions compose almost linearly (reducing the inductive demand), the other makes goals action-explicit (reducing the abductive one). In both, solve rates return to roughly the test set level, showing that either simplification alone is enough to reduce TranSGrid to an ordinary held-out test set. Together, our results show that existing tasks reduce either or both of the inductive and abductive demands, and that comprehensively measuring systematic generalization requires a task that involves all three forms of reasoning.

</details>


### [223] [Characterizing Web Search by Conversational LLM Agents: From Search Decisions and Strategies to Results and Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19244)
*Mahsa Amani,Seungeon Lee,Abhisek Dash,Asmaa El Fraihi,Yunah Jang,Elisabeth Kirsten,Qinyuan Wu,Krishna P. Gummadi,Manish Gupta,Abhilasha Ravichander,Muhammad Bilal Zafar,Soumi Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究了对话式LLM智能体（ChatGPT、Claude、Grok、DeepSeek）的Web搜索全生命周期，结合真实用户交互（invivo）与API受控实验（invitro），揭示了搜索触发决策、查询策略、领域偏好及结果转化中的差异与可靠性隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式LLM智能体日益依赖Web搜索，但其端到端的智能体搜索生命周期仍缺乏理解。作者希望填补这一空白，并同时兼顾真实使用场景与可控实验，从而为未来AI智能体和面向对话检索优化的搜索工具设计提供依据。

Method: 研究覆盖四大对话平台（ChatGPT、Claude、Grok、DeepSeek），采用两种互补方法：一是invivo的真实用户交互研究，二是invitro的、使用同一平台模型API的受控实验。分析维度包括：触发Web搜索的智能体决策质量、查询构建策略、搜索结果中的领域偏好，以及将搜索结果转化为有依据回答时的选择。

Result: Web搜索决策在不同平台与模型间差异显著，更频繁地调用Web搜索并不必然带来更好的回答质量；对话智能体采用不同的复杂查询策略；各平台特有的搜索引擎倾向于返回其偏好领域的结果；回答虽大体基于搜索结果，但仍有部分论断依赖未被引用的搜索结果，引发对归因与可靠性的担忧。

Conclusion: 该研究对面向对话检索优化的未来AI智能体与Web搜索工具设计具有重要启示，尤其强调了搜索触发时机、查询策略、领域偏差以及引用归因可靠性等关键问题。

Abstract: Conversational LLM agents increasingly rely on Web search, yet the end-to-end lifecycle of agentic search remains poorly understood. We present the first study of Web search across four major conversational platforms (ChatGPT, Claude, Grok, and DeepSeek), combining real-world user interactions (invivo) with controlled experiments using the same platform's models by their APIs (invitro). We investigate the quality of agentic decisions to invoke Web search, their strategies to formulate queries, the potential domain preferences in the search results they receive, and the choices they make when transforming search results into grounded responses. We find that Web-search decisions vary substantially across platforms and models, while more frequent Web-search invocation does not necessarily yield better response quality. We further show that conversational agents employ different complex querying strategies and that platform specific search engines return search results from their preferred domains. Finally, although responses are largely grounded in search results, some claims rely on uncited search results, raising concerns about attribution and reliability. Our findings have important implications for the design of future AI agents and Web search tools optimized for conversational retrieval.

</details>


### [224] [Do AI Agents Understand Computer Architecture?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19387)
*Ambika Sharan,Grigory Chirkov,Soheil Abbasloo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoTuring 用同一智能体、同一 15 维加速器空间做两次搜索：一次以有语义的架构旋钮+模拟器计数器呈现，一次以 [0,1] 上的匿名变量呈现，两者评估器、合法空间与可达最优完全相同，从而把"性能提升"与"是否真正理解机器"分离开来；差异本身即为测量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有报告只证明"某个设计变好了"，却无法说明原因：智能体可能真的在推理机器结构，也可能只是在不理解旋钮含义的情况下盲目搜索，而只有前者能迁移到下一个架构。已有评测无法区分这两者，因为它们固定问题框架、只改变智能体；本文反过来固定智能体、改变问题的可理解性。

Method: AutoTuring 将同一智能体、同一 15 维加速器设计空间呈现两次：(1) 具名架构旋钮并附带模拟器计数器；(2) [0,1] 区间上的匿名变量。两者的评估器、合法空间与可达最优保持一致，唯一变量是问题是否"有意义"。在九个内核的 FP16 GEMM 测试集上进行比较。

Result: 在有意义的框架下，"架构师"平均比建模的 H200 高 5.4%，比盲搜对照高 12.3%，且模拟器调用次数减少 70.1%。但意义并非唯一来源：加入 critic 循环后，盲搜智能体可挽回大部分差距，而对架构师版本几乎无增益，说明架构知识与结构化批评是替代关系而非互补关系。

Conclusion: 架构语义确实带来收益，但可用结构化批评替代，二者呈替代性。作者将结论定位为初步发现（每个条件仅 5–6 次运行，单一建模加速器），并强调贡献在于这一对比测量方法本身，而非所设计出的加速器。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly asked to design hardware, and increasingly reported to succeed. Such reports establish that a design improved; they cannot establish why. An agent that improves an accelerator may be reasoning about the machine, or may be searching competently over knobs whose meaning it never recovers -- and only the first transfers to the next architecture. Existing evaluations cannot tell the two apart, because they vary the agent while holding the framing of the problem fixed. We do the opposite. AutoTuring hands the same agent the same 15-dimensional accelerator space twice: once as named architectural knobs with simulator counters, once as anonymous variables on [0,1], with the evaluator, the legal space and the reachable optima held identical, so that the only thing that varies is whether the problem means anything. The gap between the two is the measurement. On a nine-kernel FP16 GEMM basket, meaning pays: the architect beats a modeled H200 by 5.4% and its blind counterpart by 12.3% on average, with 70.1% fewer simulator calls. It does not pay uniquely: a critic loop recovers most of that gap for the blind agent and buys the architect nothing, so architectural knowledge and structured critique behave as substitutes rather than as complements. We report these as preliminary findings -- five to six runs per condition on a single modeled accelerator -- and take the comparison itself, not the accelerator, to be the contribution.

</details>


### [225] [MAGS: Multi-agent Auto-formalization Guarantees Safety for Agentic Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19391)
*Albert Wu,Nicholas Roberts,Tzu-Heng Huang,Haoran Lin,Gil Friedman,Sungjun Cho,Gabriel Orlanski,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGS是一个多智能体框架，利用Dafny作为验证感知的中间表示，为LLM生成的程序提供可机械检查的安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体生成的复杂程序规模庞大，人工审查愈发困难，存在安全与安全隐患。模糊测试、静态分析、LLM-as-a-Verifier等方法难以覆盖所有边界情况；形式化验证虽能提供机器可检查的保证，但传统上需要大量人工规格编写与证明工程。

Method: 提出统一多智能体框架MAGS：以Dafny作为验证感知的中间表示；将人工审计过的API和安全需求形式化并冻结；把生成的代码翻译为Dafny；利用验证器反馈修复违规；最后将验证通过的程序编译回可执行代码。

Result: 在100个CUDA内核、100个终端脚本和20个机械臂任务共220个样例上，针对冻结规格实现了100%的成功率，生成具有非平凡安全保证的程序；独立的安全性与功能性评估在三个领域均表现强劲，同时揭示了当自动形式化语义未能完整刻画目标行为时出现的失败。

Conclusion: MAGS通过多智能体与验证感知中间表示的结合，使LLM生成代码在无需大量人工证明工程的情况下获得形式化安全保证，但自动形式化语义的覆盖度仍是影响其可靠性的关键局限。

Abstract: LLM coding agents now generate complex programs at a scale that makes thorough human review increasingly difficult, raising the risk of safety and security failures. Common approaches, including fuzz testing, static analysis, and LLM-as-a-Verifier, can detect many failures but struggle to cover all possible edge cases. Formal verification addresses this by providing machine-checkable guarantees over specified properties, but traditionally demands substantial manual specification and proof engineering. We introduce a unified multi-agent framework, MAGS, that generates executable programs with formal safety guarantees, using Dafny as a verification-aware intermediate representation where safety properties can be mechanically checked. MAGS formalizes and freezes human-audited APIs and safety requirements, translates generated code into Dafny, repairs violations using verifier feedback, and compiles verified programs back into executable code. We evaluate MAGS on 100 CUDA kernels, 100 terminal scripts, and 20 robotic-arm tasks. Across all 220 examples, it achieves a 100% success rate in producing programs with non-trivial safety guarantees against frozen specifications. Independent safety and functional evaluations further show strong performance across all three domains, while revealing failures when the auto-formalized semantics do not fully capture the target behavior.

</details>


### [226] [Closed-World Resolution Against Tool Hallucination in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19425)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文是一项测量与基准研究，揭示并量化了工具增强LLM智能体中"工具幻觉"（调用不存在的工具、传递未声明参数）这一结构性盲区，提出五类分类法（H1-H5）与免训练解析器Resolution Rung，并在十个托管模型及MCP场景中实测幻觉，发布HTB基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法要么做工具选择（selection），要么对真实工具的使用做门控（gating），二者都预设所发出的调用针对真实工具。然而幻觉调用在构造上并非任何门控所做出的决策，因此任何门控都无法拒绝它，这是一个结构性盲区，此前无人系统研究。

Method: 给出工具幻觉的五类分类法（H1-H5）；提出作为参照点的Resolution Rung：免训练、封闭世界的解析器（注册表成员资格检查加签名校验），重点在于它必须位于何处而非其计算内容；证明幻觉防御必须先于任何因果门控，并刻画唯一不可约的残余（借用参数在模式上与有效调用无法区分）；随后扩展到Model Context Protocol，提出第二套分类法（M1-M5）。

Result: 在十个托管模型、两种调用界面下共测得322次真实幻觉；伪造工具调用集中在无约束的raw-JSON界面（34对3）；模型规模无帮助（675B模型与7-8B模型表现相当）。在MCP场景中测得154次幻觉，包括在单注册表界面表现干净的前沿模型，因为冲突与遮蔽是合并过程的结构性问题。

Conclusion: 工具幻觉防御必须在因果门控之前进行；多服务器命名空间合并会带来单一注册表无法表达的幻觉面。作者发布带版本号的Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark（HTB），使任何解析器可在不同提交间进行比较。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model (LLM) agents fail in a way no tool-selection or tool-security method addresses: they call tools that do not exist and pass arguments no schema declares. Existing defenses either pick the right tool (selection) or constrain what an agent may do with real tools (gating), both of which presuppose the emitted call refers to a real tool at all. We show this is a structural blind spot: a hallucinated call is by construction not a decision any gate made, so no gate can reject it. This paper is primarily a measurement and benchmark study. We give a five-class taxonomy of tool hallucination (H1-H5) and, as a reference point, the Resolution Rung: a training-free, closed-world resolver (registry membership plus a signature check) whose interest is where it must sit, not what it computes. We prove hallucination defense must precede any causal gate, and characterize the one irreducible residue (borrowed arguments schema-indistinguishable from a valid call). Across ten hosted models under two invocation surfaces we measure 322 genuine hallucinations; fabricated-tool calls concentrate on the unconstrained raw-JSON surface (34 vs. 3), and model scale does not help (a 675B model matches a 7-8B one). We then extend to the Model Context Protocol, where merging several servers into one namespace creates hallucination surfaces a single registry cannot express (a second taxonomy, M1-M5); on the live MCP surface we measure 154 hallucinations, including from frontier models that were clean on the single-registry surface, because collisions and shadowing are structural to the merge. We release the versioned Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark (HTB) so any resolver is comparable across submissions.

</details>


### [227] [The syntax and semantics of goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19448)
*David M. Abel,Mark K. Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将"目标"视为具有内容的组合性表征，并借鉴语言学与逻辑学中的"句法—语义界面"视角，提出对目标表征的表达力、设计与效率等性质进行系统刻画的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在认知科学与计算机科学中，目标通常被理解为可与世界知识灵活结合、用以组织和规范有目的行为的认知状态，但对其"表征"本身的性质缺乏系统关注。作者希望通过类比语言学与逻辑学中的句法—语义界面，凸显关于目标表征的表达力、设计与效率等基础性问题，从而厘清隐含假设并整合分散的研究工作。

Method: 本文为概念性/综述性研究，不涉及实验。作者通过梳理与综合已有关于不同目标表征的研究，将目标的形式与意义加以区分，并把不同目标表征的性质（如表达力是否足以捕捉所关注的行为、不同表征是否刻画同一行为）定位为一个更广泛"设计空间"中的不同维度。

Result: 作者指出，目标表征通常被视为固定且对期望行为施加约束，但同样可以识别出施加于目标表征本身的约束，例如某种目标语言是否具有足够表达力、不同目标表征是否对应同一行为。这些性质构成了目标表征的统一设计空间，可被系统比较。

Conclusion: 区分目标的形式与意义有助于澄清我们对目标所作的隐含假设，促进对高层认知与动机之间交互的研究，并为不同目标概念隔离出可比较的变异维度。

Abstract: In both cognitive science and computer science, goals are conceptualized as cognitive states that flexibly combine with world knowledge to organize and specify purposeful behavior. In this way, goals are compositional representations whose content relates to rational behavior. We here draw attention to goals as representations and their content because it highlights a parallel with other areas in cognitive science - in particular, the syntax-semantics interface in linguistics and logic - while also foregrounding foundational questions about the expressivity, design, and efficiency of different goal representations. For example, goals are typically taken as fixed and imposing constraints on desirable behaviors, but we can also identify constraints on goal representations themselves, such as whether a particular goal language is sufficiently expressive to capture behaviors of interest, or whether different goal representations capture the same behavior. Here, we synthesize work that aims to characterize the properties of different goal representations and suggest these are points of a broader design space. We close by discussing how distinguishing the form and meaning of goals can elucidate the implicit assumptions we make about goals, inform the study of interactions between higher-level cognition and motivation, and isolate axes of variation for different conceptions of goals.

</details>


### [228] [Compositional Reasoning in Language Models under Reinforcement Learning Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19465)
*Yu He,Yingxi Li,Yifei Wang,Ellen Vitercik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出依赖图框架形式化组合推理，分为三个递增复杂度层级；通过数据结构任务发现"分解到组合"的不对称性：分解技能训练难以迁移到组合任务，反之则较易。并给出理论解释与多维度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实问题求解依赖组合推理，因为训练数据有限，模型必须通过以新方式组合已学技能来泛化。尽管RL等后训练方法显著提升了语言模型推理能力，但其对组合推理的影响仍不明确。

Method: 提出依赖图框架形式化组合推理，定义三个复杂度递增的组合性层级；用具有确定性奖励和清晰组合结构的数据结构任务实例化该框架；进行理论分析解释不对称性；评估长度外推、结构分布偏移、未见技能迁移；并在真实工具调用基准上做初步研究。

Result: 发现一致的"分解到组合"不对称性：分解技能训练不能可靠迁移到组合任务，而组合任务训练更易迁移回分解任务；理论解释了该不对称性；长度外推、结构分布偏移和未见技能迁移下的组合泛化得到评估；工具调用基准初步证据显示该不对称性可延伸到实际场景。

Conclusion: 组合推理存在方向性不对称，组合训练比分解训练更具泛化优势；该现象在受控数据结构任务和实际工具调用场景中均有体现。

Abstract: Compositional reasoning is critical for real-world problem solving: since training data is necessarily limited, models must generalize by composing learned skills in new ways. While post-training methods such as reinforcement learning (RL) have substantially improved the reasoning abilities of language models (LMs), their effects on compositional reasoning remain less well understood. We propose a dependency-graph framework to formalize compositional reasoning, yielding three levels of compositionality with increasing complexity. Empirically, we instantiate this framework with data-structure tasks, which provide deterministic reward computation and clear compositional structure. We find a consistent decomposed-to-composed asymmetry: decomposed-skill training does not reliably transfer to composed tasks, whereas composed-task training transfers more readily back to decomposed tasks. We provide theoretical explanation for this asymmetry, and further evaluate compositional generalization under length extrapolation, structural distribution shift, and transfer to tasks requiring unseen skills. Finally, we present a pilot study on real-world tool-calling benchmarks, showing preliminary evidence that the decomposed-to-composed asymmetry can extend to practical settings.

</details>


### [229] [Safety Beyond the Interface: Detecting Harm via Latent States in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19472)
*Alizishaan Khatri,Chiquita Prabhu,Omkar Neogi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过提取LLaMA-3.1-8B内部激活训练轻量MLP探针（12.6M参数）检测有害提示，效果接近千倍大的外部守护模型，同时大幅降低延迟与算力开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统日益依赖大语言模型，但现有外部守护模型引入延迟与算力开销，难以用于资源受限、时间敏感场景；且外部模型无法感知模型内部状态，存在根本性保障缺口。作者提出疑问：模型内部是否已经"知道"内容有害？

Method: 从LLaMA-3.1-8B中提取激活值，训练轻量级MLP分类器探针（约12.6M参数）来检测有害提示，利用模型内部表征而非外部独立模型进行安全判定。

Result: 在WildJailbreak、Beavertails和AEGIS 2.0三个数据集上，探针F1分数分别为99%、83%和84%，与参数量大1000倍的守护模型相比具有竞争力，同时显著降低延迟和计算成本。

Conclusion: 模型内部激活已蕴含有害性判别信息，轻量探针可作为高效内部守护方案，在保持竞争性安全性能的同时缓解资源受限场景下的延迟与算力瓶颈。

Abstract: Autonomous systems increasingly rely on Large Language Models (LLMs) yet the safety infrastructure surrounding these models introduces latency and compute overhead. This limits utility in resource-constrained, time-critical deployments. Existing external guardrail models remain blind to the model's internal workings, creating a fundamental assurance gap. We ask: does the model already know when the content is harmful? We extract activations from LLaMA-3.1-8B and train lightweight MLP classifier probes (12.6M parameters) to detect harmful prompts. Evaluated on WildJailbreak, Beavertails, and AEGIS 2.0, our probes achieve F1 scores of 99%, 83%, and 84%, respectively competitive with 1000x larger guard models while cutting latency and compute costs.

</details>


### [230] [QVAC Genesis III: A Large-Scale, High-Quality Open Synthetic STEM Corpus for Efficient Language Model Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19513)
*Davide Vitabile,N. Ranjan,Akshay Nambiar,Kamal K. Gupta,Amril Nazir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出QVAC Genesis III，一个191.43B token的STEM多领域合成语料库，面向边缘AI小模型，采用师生蒸馏双生成策略，并配LLM-as-a-parser评测协议，在1.7B模型上验证其显著优于现有开源合成语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI和端侧部署对token预算有严格限制，高质量预训练数据成为瓶颈。大机构使用私有语料训练大模型，而开放生态缺乏面向STEM、能为小模型提供高"每token学习价值"的合成数据集，存在明显空白。

Method: 构建191.43B token、覆盖19个STEM领域、多难度等级与多种教学风格的合成语料；采用双生成策略：以弱边缘规模学生模型作为信号进行定向教师蒸馏，将其失败转化为纠正性解释，将其成功扩展为覆盖所有选项的对比式选项级推理；同时提出LLM-as-a-parser评测协议，从自由文本输出中提取最终答案，并同时追踪准确率与答案有效性。

Result: 在1.7B参数模型上做从零训练的受控消融实验，使用QVAC Genesis III训练的模型在ARC、GPQA Diamond和MMLU STEM基准上持续优于使用开源合成语料Cosmopedia-v2训练的模型以及公开的Cosmo-1B模型，ARC-E最高提升+28.57%，ARC-C最高提升+21.35%，有效答案率最高达99.45%。

Conclusion: QVAC Genesis III证明面向STEM的高质量合成数据能显著提升小模型的学习效率与性能，为token预算受限的边缘部署场景提供了有效的开放数据方案。

Abstract: High-quality pre-training data is a critical bottleneck for educational and STEM-specific language models targeting edge AI and on-device deployment where token budgets are tightly constrained. While major organizations train ever-larger models on private corpora, the open ecosystem lacks STEM-focused synthetic datasets that deliver high per-token learning value efficiently for small models. To address this gap, we introduce QVAC Genesis III, a 191.43B-token, STEM-focused multi-domain synthetic corpus covering 19 domains across several difficulty levels and different educational styles. QVAC Genesis III is built via a dual generation strategy that performs targeted teacher distillation using a weak edge-scale student model as signal: the student's failures are converted into corrective explanations, while its successes are expanded into contrastive option-level reasoning over all answer choices. We further introduce an LLM-as-a-parser evaluation protocol that extracts final answers from free-form outputs and tracks both accuracy and answer validity. To validate the effectiveness of our QVAC Genesis III data, we conduct controlled from-scratch ablations with 1.7B-parameter models, showing that models trained with QVAC Genesis III consistently outperform both models trained with the open-source synthetic corpus Cosmopedia-v2 and the publicly released Cosmo-1B model across ARC, GPQA Diamond, and MMLU STEM benchmarks, achieving up to +28.57% on ARC-E and +21.35% on ARC-C, while reaching a Valid Answer Rate of up to 99.45%.

</details>


### [231] [LLM-as-an-Improver: Turning Verification into Better Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19515)
*Akiyoshi Tomihari,Yuma Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-as-an-Improver与Verify–Repair–Reselect (VRR)框架，将验证反馈用于生成并重选更优候选，而非仅做排序，从而突破固定候选池的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于验证器的选择方法只把验证当作排序步骤，一旦固定候选池被评估完就丢弃验证反馈，无法改进候选集本身。作者因此探究验证能否也提升候选集合的质量。

Method: 提出VRR方法：保留初始最优解，并条件性地生成三个互补备选——最优解与次优解的修复版本，以及一个基于新思路的解；仅用推理时信息过滤无效与重复候选，再按原始评价标准重选最终答案。

Result: 在多种模型及代码生成与推理基准上，VRR在多个设置中优于固定池的验证器选择，并且即使初始池中所有候选都错误，也能恢复出正确解。

Conclusion: 验证反馈不仅能从既有解中选择，还能构造超越初始池的更强候选，凸显了LLM作为改进者的更广泛作用。

Abstract: Verifier-based selection improves LLM performance by generating multiple candidate solutions and using a verifier to select the most promising one. However, existing methods typically treat verification only as a ranking step and discard its feedback once a fixed candidate pool has been evaluated. In this paper, we ask whether verification can also improve the candidate set itself. To this end, we introduce LLM-as-an-Improver and propose Verify--Repair--Reselect (VRR), which uses verification feedback to generate and reselect improved candidates. VRR retains the initial winner while conditionally generating three complementary alternatives: repaired versions of the winner and runner-up, and a solution based on a new approach. It filters invalid and duplicate candidates using only inference-time information and then reselects the final answer under the original evaluation criteria. Across diverse models and code-generation and reasoning benchmarks, VRR improves over fixed-pool verifier-based selection in many settings and can recover correct solutions even when all candidates in the initial pool are incorrect. These results highlight a broader role for LLMs as improvers: verification feedback can not only select among existing solutions but also construct stronger candidates beyond the initial pool.

</details>


### [232] [An Architecture for Long-Horizon Agents: Levels, Ticks and Cascaded Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19519)
*Erik Nijkamp,Anurag Koul,Egor Pakhomov,Bo Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 长时程语言模型智能体需要"持续运行而不遗忘"的能力，而这种能力来自模型外部的harness而非模型本身。作者提出三层级架构（时间尺度层级、时钟式tick、级联智能），并用十天实验验证了跨上下文重置的连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型智能体被要求承担跨越数天甚至数周的任务（如运维修复或研究项目），这类任务远超任何上下文窗口、任何进程和人类可关注的间隔。作者认为长时程智能体必须先做到持续运行且不遗忘，才谈得上持续学习。

Method: 作者从长时程场景中提炼出七个瓶颈，并用三部分层级架构回应：(i) 按时间尺度索引的层级，每层维护一份对下层有界摘要的文件；(ii) 以时钟式tick作为自主行动单元；(iii) 级联智能，即工作只有在复核失败后才升级给更强的模型。这些机制位于模型外部的harness中。

Result: 作者进行了为期十天的实验，智能体在该架构上复现了一个已发表的强化学习结果，期间人类每天仅关注一次。结果显示：(1) 智能体在campaign中每次上下文重置和会话边界处都保持了任务线索；(2) 早期写入的操作性知识改变了后续行为，且未改动模型权重；(3) 明确了学习组件应进入系统的位置。

Conclusion: 作者的经验表明，这类智能体的持续学习需要一个能够存续于每次上下文和进程之外的基础载体，而harness已经在运行的检查环节正是学习器应当安放的位置。

Abstract: Language-model agents are increasingly asked to carry out work spanning days or weeks, such as an operations remediation or a research programme. Such a task outlives any context window, any process and any interval at which a person can attend. In this paper, we argue that a long-horizon agent must run continually without forgetting before it can learn continually. This ability lies in the harness around the model rather than in the model itself. We derive seven bottlenecks from the long-horizon setting and answer them with a hierarchical architecture of three parts: (i) levels indexed by time scale, each keeping a bounded file summarising the level below; (ii) a clocked tick as the unit of autonomous action; and (iii) cascaded intelligence, where work is escalated to a more capable model only after failing review. We report on a ten-day campaign in which an agent built on this architecture reproduced a published reinforcement-learning result with a human attending once a day, and show (1) the agent kept the thread across every context reset and session boundary of the campaign, (2) operating knowledge written early changed later behaviour with no change to model weights, and (3) where learned components would enter such a system. Overall, our experience suggests continual learning for these agents needs a substrate outliving every context and process, and the checks the harness already runs are where a learner belongs.

</details>


### [233] [EconSkills: Studying Skill Transfer and Retrieval for Web Agents on Live Economic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19523)
*Yinzhu Quan,Zefang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EconSkills：一个将 EconWebArena 成功轨迹蒸馏为参数化标准操作流程（SOP）的技能库与评测框架，验证了可复用经济网页操作流程能跨任务实例迁移，并为覆盖率感知的技能选择与上下文交付提供设计目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网页智能体常重复访问相同站点，但多数评测会丢弃早期成功交互中习得的操作流程，导致经验无法复用；同时需要区分"已知相关流程能否迁移到新任务"与"智能体能否从技能库中选择并保留该收益"这两个问题。

Method: 构建 EconSkills 技能库：把已验证的 EconWebArena 轨迹蒸馏为参数化标准操作流程，每条技能记录适用范围、导航流程、站点特定指引、验证检查与恢复步骤，并将来源实例的具体取值替换为占位符。评测分为受控迁移实验（对比匹配技能、无技能提示、重放原始轨迹）与库规模检索实验，并进行覆盖率分层分析与浏览器轨迹分析。

Result: 受控迁移中，匹配技能相比无技能提示提升成功率，并在成对成功案例中减少所需步骤；抽象化显著优于重放原始轨迹。库规模下，检索整体与无技能基线相当，在直接覆盖任务上表现最佳；覆盖率分层显示未覆盖任务上的近似匹配抵消了部分收益。浏览器轨迹进一步识别出流程指引能缩短特定门户导航的时机，以及语义验证仍属必要的场景。

Conclusion: 可复用的经济类网页操作流程能够跨任务实例迁移，为覆盖率感知的技能选择与上下文交付提供了具体的设计目标。

Abstract: Web agents often revisit the same sites, yet most evaluations discard the procedures learned in earlier successful interactions. We introduce EconSkills, a skill library and evaluation framework that distills verified EconWebArena trajectories into parameterized standard operating procedures for retrieving live economic data. Each skill records its scope, navigation procedure, site-specific guidance, verification checks, and recovery steps while replacing source-instance values with placeholders. EconSkills separates two questions: whether a known relevant procedure transfers to a held-out task, and whether an agent can retain that benefit when selecting from a library. In controlled transfer, matched skills improve success over no-skill prompting and require fewer steps on paired successes, while abstraction is substantially more effective than replaying raw trajectories. At library scale, retrieval is competitive with the no-skill baseline overall and performs best on directly covered tasks; coverage-stratified outcomes show that approximate matches on uncovered tasks offset these gains. Browser trajectories further identify when procedural guidance shortens portal-specific navigation and when semantic verification remains necessary. These results establish that reusable economic web procedures can transfer across task instances and provide a concrete design target for coverage-aware selection and context delivery.

</details>


### [234] [A Unified Evaluation Framework for Trustworthy Large Language Models, Agentic AI, and Multimodal Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19524)
*Shaina Raza,Ahmed Y. Radwan,Imran Liaquat,Kathryn Hume*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的可信度评估框架，将输出级、轨迹级和跨模态评估与八个可信度维度相连接，并加入元评估层以保证评估证据本身的可解释性与可信性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠基准分数无法完整评估现代AI系统（LLM、智能体系统、多模态模型）的可信度；不同系统需要不同的评估方式，但评估证据必须保持可解释，以支撑开发与监督。

Method: 构建统一框架，以八个可信度维度（能力、鲁棒性、安全、公平、透明、治理、监督、效率）贯通输出级、轨迹级与跨模态评估；保留各系统特有指标，将原生测量映射到统一性能区间，并附不确定度估计和可追溯证据；设立元评估层检验评估本身的有效性、可靠性与可复现性；通过多维画像呈现优劣，并以安全关键覆盖机制防止聚合分数掩盖关键失效；映射至治理框架、国际标准和欧盟监管要求。

Result: 框架能够同时评估系统性能及其证据的可信度，提供统一性能区间、不确定度与可追溯证据，并通过多维画像与安全关键覆盖避免聚合分数掩盖严重问题。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估AI系统性能及支撑证据的可信度提供了结构化基础，连接技术评估与监督需求；在不同部署情境下的实证验证仍是下一步必要工作。

Abstract: Benchmark scores alone provide an incomplete basis for assessing the trustworthiness of modern artificial intelligence systems. Large language models (LLMs), agentic systems, and multimodal models (MLLMs) require different forms of assessment, yet their evaluation evidence must remain interpretable for development and oversight. We propose a unified framework that connects output-level, trajectory-level, and cross-modal assessment through eight trustworthiness dimensions: capability, robustness, safety, fairness, transparency, governance, oversight, and efficiency. The framework preserves system-specific metrics while mapping native measurements to common performance bands, accompanied by uncertainty estimates and traceable evidence. A meta-evaluation layer examines the validity, reliability, and reproducibility of the evaluation itself. Multidimensional profiles expose strengths and weaknesses, while safety-critical overrides prevent aggregate scores from masking critical failures. Mappings to governance frameworks, international standards, and European Union regulatory requirements connect technical assessment with oversight needs. The framework provides a structured basis for assessing both system performance and the credibility of the evidence supporting it, with empirical validation across deployment contexts remaining an essential next step.

</details>


### [235] [Self Improvement via Fast Tree-search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19526)
*Xinghong Fu,Aravinth Kulanthaivelu,Yutaro Yamada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIFT：一种样本高效、低成本的编码代理递归自我改进框架，通过LLM裁判信号与分解式树搜索减少昂贵评估开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理可递归修改自身实现形成自我改进循环，但已有方法成本高、计算密集。本文希望在实际预算限制下高效提升编码性能，并识别出候选自我修改的评估（需重跑基准任务子集）是主要运行时瓶颈。

Method: 提出SIFT（Recursive Self Improvement via Fast Tree-search）。在下游任务评估之外引入LLM-as-a-judge，对候选补丁进行成对比较；用正则化Bradley-Terry模型聚合胜负记录得到强度分数，据此在轻量分解式树搜索中进行基于排名的父节点采样；只对最有前景节点执行昂贵的下游任务评估。

Result: 在完整Polyglot基准上，SIFT优于现有基于树搜索的自我进化框架，并在CPU小时、墙钟时间与API成本方面资源需求显著更低。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM裁判作为中间信号引导探索，并用分解式树搜索避开慢速评估瓶颈，可在严格预算下实现样本高效的编码代理递归自我改进。

Abstract: Coding agents can recursively modify their own implementations, forming a loop of self-improvement. While prior work shows this can boost performance on coding benchmarks, existing approaches are costly and compute-intensive. We introduce a simple, sample-efficient self-improvement framework that significantly improves coding performance under strict budget constraints. We identify evaluation of candidate self-modifications as the main runtime bottleneck since prior approaches estimate their effectiveness by re-running a subset of benchmark tasks with the modified agent, which is time-consuming. We introduce Recursive Self Improvement via Fast Tree-search (SIFT), which augments these downstream task evaluations with an LLM-as-a-judge signal that performs pairwise comparisons between candidate patches, where the win-loss record is aggregated with a regularized Bradley-Terry model, and the resulting strength scores drive rank-based parent sampling inside a lightweight disaggregated tree search. Expensive downstream task evaluations are reserved only for the most promising nodes. Using a fully disaggregated tree search pipeline, the judge scores provide intermediate signal to guide exploration on promising candidate patches without being bottlenecked by slow evaluation runs. SIFT outperforms existing tree-search based self-evolution frameworks on the full Polyglot benchmark with significantly lower resource requirements in terms of CPU hours, wall clock time, and API cost.

</details>


### [236] [When Hiring Becomes Agent-Mediated: Evaluating Access and Recurrence in Two-Agent Résumé Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19530)
*Jian Gao,Hang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将简历筛选建模为雇主侧与候选侧两个智能体交换证据、更新判断后再决定是否推进，发现双智能体筛选比一次性静态判断让更多申请通过，但并非简单放宽标准，而是改变决策方向与通过对象，且其独有选中项在不同重跑间复现性较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 招聘本质是双向过程：雇主评估匹配度，候选人展示并辩护自身资历。然而作为第一道关卡的简历筛选，通常被自动化建模为对简历-职位配对的静态一次性判断，忽略了这种交互性。作者希望探究当雇主侧与候选侧智能体分别代表双方角色、交换证据并更新判断时，筛选程序本身会如何影响谁进入人工复审以及这种准入的可复现性。

Method: 研究对比两类程序：一是传统的一次性静态判断，二是双智能体筛选（雇主侧与候选侧智能体交换证据并更新判断后再决定推进）。在600个构造的简历-职位配对上使用GPT-5.5与Claude Opus 4.7进行实验，并在191对边界样本池上各运行三次，比较通过实例比率、决策方向变化、通过对象重叠度，以及在全新运行中重新执行被选中案例的复现情况。

Result: 双智能体筛选让更多申请推进：GPT-5.5从33.3%升至39.3%，Opus 4.7从34.0%升至35.5%。在191对边界池三次运行中，通过实例比率分别由4.5%升至26.2%、6.5%升至16.1%。但这不是一致性放宽：双智能体筛选会拒绝一次性判断通过的申请，决策双向变化；在相近通过量下两种程序通过不同申请，且没有任何一次性阈值能稳定复现双智能体筛选的选人结果。在全新运行中重执行的案例里，仅双智能体选中的案例复现率低于共有选中案例（GPT-5.5下明显，Opus 4.7下较弱），而单独的一次性跟进未显示类似下降。

Conclusion: 当招聘双方都由智能体代理时，决定谁进入人工复审及其准入可复现性的，不仅是背后的模型，筛选程序本身也是关键因素。双智能体筛选改变了决策方向与通过对象，且其独有选择的可复现性较弱，提示筛选流程设计需被纳入评估与责任考量。

Abstract: Hiring is bilateral: employers assess fit, while candidates present and defend evidence of their qualifications. Yet résumé screening, the first gate, is commonly automated as a static, one-call judgment over a résumé-job pair. We study a two-agent alternative in which employer-side and candidate-side agents represent these roles, exchange evidence, and update their judgments before deciding who advances. We compare procedures on 600 constructed résumé-job pairs using GPT-5.5 and Claude Opus 4.7. Two-agent screening advances more applications (33.3% to 39.3% for GPT-5.5; 34.0% to 35.5% for Opus 4.7). Across three runs on the common 191-pair borderline pool, pass-instance rates rise from 4.5% to 26.2% and from 6.5% to 16.1%, respectively. This is not a uniform relaxation: two-agent screening rejects applications one-call advances, changing decisions in both directions. At similar pass volumes, the procedures advance different applications, and no one-call threshold recovers applications consistently selected by two-agent screening. Among discovery-selected cases re-executed in fresh runs, two-agent-only selections recur less often than shared selections, clearly under GPT-5.5 and less certainly under Opus 4.7, while a separate one-call follow-up shows no comparable decline. As hiring becomes agent-mediated on both sides, the screening procedure, not only the model behind it, shapes who reaches human review and how reliably that access recurs.

</details>


### [237] [Agentic AI Networking for Heterogeneous Unmanned Aerial Systems in Low-Altitude Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19538)
*Nguyen Duc Minh Quang,Chang Liu,Shuangyang Li,Derrick Wing Kwan Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种分层混合LLM-MARL双环架构，实现低空无线网络中异构服务的自主协调与动态目标重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无线网络（LAWNs）中异构无人系统的共存使移动性、连接性与共享资源强耦合，异构服务需求各异且时变，形成动态非合作博弈。传统优化与基于学习的控制器依赖预定义目标，难以自主适应不断变化的服务需求和资源优先级。

Method: 提出分层混合LLM-MARL架构，采用双环结构：外环为LLM辅助的博弈编排，用于解读服务需求与运营方意图，并重构目标与资源优先级；内环则在配置好的博弈下执行去中心化、参数条件化的MARL策略。

Result: 以物流监控案例研究展示了该框架如何促进异构服务间的协调共存，并能在无需重新训练底层MARL策略的情况下适应不断变化的运行条件。

Conclusion: 讨论面向可扩展、可信且自适应的智能体化LAWNs的关键挑战与研究方向。

Abstract: Low-altitude wireless networks (LAWNs) are emerging as a key infrastructure for heterogeneous unmanned aerial systems that support concurrent services within a shared three-dimensional airspace. Their coexistence creates strong coupling among mobility, connectivity, and shared network resources, while heterogeneous services impose distinct and time-varying requirements. These interactions naturally form a dynamic non-cooperative game in which both operating conditions and coordination objectives evolve over time. Conventional optimization and learning-based controllers typically rely on predefined objectives, limiting their ability to adapt autonomously to changing service requirements and resource priorities. To address this challenge, we propose a hierarchical hybrid large language model (LLM)- multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) architecture organized as a dual-loop structure. Specifically, an outer adaptation loop employs LLM-assisted game orchestration to interpret service requirements and operator intent, and reconfigure objectives and resource priorities, while an inner loop executes decentralized, parameter-conditioned MARL policies under the configured game. A logistics-monitoring case study illustrates how the proposed framework facilitates coordinated coexistence among heterogeneous services, adapting to evolving operating conditions without retraining the underlying MARL policies. Finally, we discuss key challenges and research directions toward scalable, trustworthy, and adaptive agentic LAWNs.

</details>


### [238] [Continual Enterprise World Model Discovery in Dynamic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19551)
*Shambhavi Mishra,David Vazquez,Perouz Taslakian,Marco Pedersoli,Jose Dolz,Issam H. Laradji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究"持续式企业世界模型发现"：在业务规则由各组织自行编写且不断变化的系统中，智能体通过交互观察发现规则、构建并持续修正世界模型。作者提出 EnterpriseWorldShift 基准（基于真实 ServiceNow，含 9 张表、25 条隐藏规则、600 个评估动作，分四个版本依次测试发现、修正、扩展与退役），并提出 Continual Discovery Agent（CDA），可跨世界携带模型，预测精度比逐题查询的先前方法最多高 8.98 IoU 点，且无需查询运行中的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业系统中更新一个字段可能触发其他字段变更、记录创建或审批流程，这些效果由各组织自行编写且随时间修订的业务规则产生，并非平台内置。因此智能体在不了解这些规则的前提下无法预测自身动作的后果。现有工作（逐题查询规则）缺乏对规则变化的持续适应能力，故需要一个考察规则发现、修正、扩展与退役的持续学习评测环境。

Method: 1) 形式化"持续式企业世界模型发现"任务：智能体初始不知规则，通过与企业记录交互并观察结果来发现规则，据此构建世界模型并在规则变化时修正它。2) 构建 EnterpriseWorldShift 基准：基于真实 ServiceNow 环境，包含 9 张表、25 条隐藏规则、600 个评估动作；提供同一企业世界的四个版本，表与记录固定不变，规则依次经历"被修改→被新增→被删除"，分别测试发现、修正、扩展与退役能力。3) 提出 Continual Discovery Agent（CDA）：构建世界模型并将其从上一个世界延续到下一个世界。

Result: CDA 对隐藏规则效果的预测比"每问一次查一次"的先前方法最多高出 8.98 个 IoU 点，并且能够完全基于自身模型作答，而无需查询正在运行的系统。

Conclusion: 结果表明，显式构建并跨任务延续世界模型，比反复查询运行系统更高效、更准确，为在企业这类规则不断演化的系统中长期自主运行的智能体提供了可行路径与评测基础。

Abstract: In an enterprise system, updating one field can set another, create a record, or start an approval. These effects are produced by business rules that are not built into the platform but written by each organization and revised over time. An agent working in such a system cannot predict the result of its own actions without knowing these rules. We study continual enterprise world model discovery, where an agent starts without knowledge of these business rules and discovers them by interacting with records and observing the outcomes. From those observations it builds a world model, which it revises as the rules change. To evaluate this, we introduce EnterpriseWorldShift, built on a live ServiceNow environment with nine tables, 25 hidden rules and 600 evaluation actions. It presents four versions of the same enterprise world, with the tables and records held fixed while a rule is modified, then added, then removed, so that discovery, revision, extension and retirement are each tested in turn. Our Continual Discovery Agent (CDA) builds such a model and carries it from one world to the next. It predicts the effects of the hidden rules more accurately than looking them up for each question, the approach taken by prior work, by up to 8.98 IoU points, and it answers from its own model without querying the running system.

</details>


### [239] [SIMLIFE: Pattern Understanding for Long-Horizon Human-Agent Partnership](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19610)
*Run Peng,Zinnia Nie,Jing Ding,Yinpei Dai,Yichi Zhang,Zengqing Wu,Yao Fu,Ziqiao Ma,Jiayuan Mao,Joyce Chai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SimLife平台与SimLife-BP基准，用于评测智能体从长期日常观察中推断潜在行为规则的能力，发现现有模型长上下文模式理解仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人类需要超越即时需求推断，捕捉惯例如何形成、为何重复以及何时改变。然而现有基准缺乏长时程、富含视觉与行为日志的仿真环境来评测这种长上下文模式理解能力。

Method: 构建SimLife可扩展平台，模拟长期家庭生活，提供丰富视觉观察、真值动作日志及带音频的合成对话；基于该平台提出SimLife-BP基准，包含106个片段（平均15.49小时、38.57游戏日）与1439个问答对，任务覆盖直接、反事实、噪声与逆向推理，并设置不同规则提示水平；对前沿模型与架构进行评测。

Result: 现有模型常仅达到表层预测而缺乏全面规则理解，依赖频率启发式而非基于证据的if-then推理，且在行为模式变化时难以适应。

Conclusion: 长上下文模式理解仍是未来具身智能体的主要瓶颈；SimLife为日常人机交互中的记忆、个性化、适应与长时程规划研究开辟了更广阔空间。

Abstract: Understanding humans over long horizons requires agents to infer not only what people need in the moment, but also how routines form, why they repeat, and when they change. We introduce SimLife, a scalable platform for simulating long-term household life with rich visual observations, ground-truth action logs, and synthetic dialogues with audio. Built on SimLife, SimLife-BP evaluates long-context pattern understanding: the ability to infer latent behavioral rules from weeks or months of everyday observations. The benchmark contains 106 episodes averaging 15.49 hours and 38.57 in-game days, and 1,439 question-answer pairs. Each task probes direct, counterfactual, noisy, and inverse reasoning under different levels of rule hints. Evaluating frontier models and architectures, we find that current models often achieve surface-level prediction without comprehensive rule understanding, rely on frequency-based heuristics rather than if-then reasoning over evidence, and struggle to adapt when behavioral patterns change. These findings suggest that long-context pattern understanding remains a major bottleneck for future embodied agents, while SimLife opens a broader space for studying memory, personalization, adaptation, and long-horizon planning in everyday human-AI interaction.

</details>


### [240] [From Intent to Action: Benchmarking LLM Safety in Vehicle Voice Command Authorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19630)
*Diba Afroze,Xingli Zhang,Yazhou Tu,Xiali Hei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个202场景的车辆语音助手授权决策基准，含七类决策标签，评测5个LLM；对齐率从40.1%到89.1%，最佳模型仍有2–3次误执行，说明结构化决策无法单独作为安全机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多地用于车载语音助手，但将自然语言请求映射到车辆功能构成安全攸关的授权问题。执行前系统需在"执行、拒绝、澄清、要求确认、转人工、触发紧急响应、不调用工具"之间抉择。已有评测未在说话人角色、认证状态、车辆状态和工具可用性等维度上孤立考察这一"行动前决策"，故需专门基准。

Method: 构建含202个场景的基准，提供七类分类体系下的参考决策。评测2个本地开源权重模型与3个API式LLM，采用决策对齐度（Decision Alignment）与安全专用错误指标。另对Llama 3.2 3B做受控消融，比较结构化授权策略与仅模式、通用安全基线。

Result: 对齐度从Llama 3.2 3B的40.1%到Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview的89.1%。API类模型在83.2%–89.1%之间，彼此无统计显著差异。即便最优模型在161个非执行场景中仍出现2–3次误执行（False Execute），确认与手工控制决策上存在持续性错误。Llama 3.2 3B消融显示：结构化授权策略下对齐度为40.1%，高于仅模式与通用安全基线的28.2%–29.2%，但未消除误执行。

Conclusion: 结构化的LLM决策不足以作为独立安全机制。实际部署需要额外的独立强制执行层，在调用任何车辆功能前校验工具权限与车辆状态约束。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into vehicle voice assistants. But linking natural-language requests to vehicle functions creates a safety-critical authorization problem. Before executing a command, the system must choose whether to execute, refuse, clarify, require confirmation, defer to manual control, trigger an emergency response, or make no tool call. To our knowledge, prior evaluations do not isolate this pre-action decision across speaker role, authentication status, vehicle state, and tool availability. We introduce a 202-scenario benchmark with Reference Decisions under a seven-class taxonomy. We evaluate two local open-weight models and three API-based LLMs using Decision Alignment and safety-specific error metrics. Alignment ranges from 40.1% for Llama 3.2 3B to 89.1% for Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview. The API-based models score between 83.2% and 89.1%, with no statistically significant differences among them. Even these models produce two to three False Executes among 161 non-execution scenarios, and persistent errors remain in confirmation and manual-control decisions. A controlled Llama 3.2 3B ablation increases alignment to 40.1% under the structured authorization policy, versus 28.2-29.2% under schema-only and generic-safety baselines, but it does not eliminate False Executes. Structured LLM decisions are therefore insufficient as a standalone safety mechanism, and deployment requires an independent enforcement layer that verifies tool permissions and vehicle-state constraints before invoking any vehicle function.

</details>


### [241] [Reach or Solve? Attributing Agentic RL Gains with Checkpoint Handoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19636)
*Xuan Liu,Jingbin Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出checkpoint handoff评估协议，将强化学习端点成功率增益分解为REACH（到达成功前固定步数的状态）和SOLVE（从克隆状态完成任务）两部分，揭示长期评估中被混淆的两类变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练的智能体在闭环环境中会因自身动作塑造后续观测状态，导致SFT与RL检查点即使在相同任务上也从不同状态被评分，端点成功率混合了"到达何处"和"到达后如何行动"两种变化；而限制比较到两策略共同到达的状态会因选择结果而翻转效应符号，无法分离二者。

Method: 提出checkpoint handoff评估协议：将一个已发布检查点到达的状态克隆并交给另一个检查点，无需重新训练；将reacher角色与solver角色在SFT和RL间交叉，并通过独立测量的REACH与SOLVE差距预测聚合交互效应。

Result: 在两个基准和两个独立发布的流程中，reacher×solver交互在全部五种条件下均为正；RL历史对RL solver的价值高于对SFT solver；在ALFWorld上RL同时改善两项指标，且SFT solver从未在RL solver失败处成功；独立的REACH与SOLVE差距可预测聚合交互。

Conclusion: Handoff仅要求一个检查点的历史能在另一检查点下重放，因此长期评估可在端点成功率之外同时报告到达率与完成率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning now trains language-model agents that act over dozens of steps in live environments. The gains are large, and they are read as better decision-making. An agent in a closed loop writes its own inputs. Each observation follows from its own earlier actions, so the states it meets late in an episode are partly of its own making. An SFT checkpoint and an RL checkpoint are then scored from different states, even on identical tasks. Endpoint success mixes two changes: where the agent arrives, and what it does once it is there. Restricting the comparison to states both policies reach does not separate them. That restriction selects on an outcome, and in our data it flips the sign of the effect. We introduce checkpoint handoff, an evaluation protocol that clones a state one released checkpoint reached and hands it to another, with no retraining. Crossing a reacher role and a solver role over SFT and RL splits an endpoint gain into REACH and SOLVE. REACH is how often a policy arrives at a state the environment confirms is a fixed number of actions from success. SOLVE is how often it finishes from an identical cloned state. Across two benchmarks and two independently released pipelines, the reacher by solver interaction is positive in all five conditions. An RL history is worth more to an RL solver than the same history is to an SFT solver. On ALFWorld, RL improves both terms, and the SFT solver never succeeds where the RL solver fails. Independent REACH and SOLVE gaps predict the aggregate interaction. Handoff asks only that one checkpoint's history can be replayed under another, so long-horizon evaluation can report arrival and completion beside endpoint success.

</details>


### [242] [ScientistTwo: Pioneering the Human Knowledge Frontier with Autonomous AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19644)
*Jaehyun Nam,Jinsung Yoon,Yanzhou Pan,Yubo Wang,Rui Meng,Parthasarathy Ranganathan,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出全自主多智能体框架 ScientistTwo，可从单个基础问题出发完成从基线建立、假设生成到实验验证与论文撰写的端到端科研循环，产出可发表论文与可执行代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现的核心在于识别现有知识边界并迈向未知领域。AI 在科学中的终极愿景是"问题驱动的自主研究"：由人类专家给出根本性挑战，AI 自主导航科研图景、发现理论与实证瓶颈并系统性地拓展知识前沿。现有工作多为辅助工具，尚难实现这一愿景。

Method: ScientistTwo 是一个全自主多智能体框架：以初始问题为输入，建立当前最优基线、提出新颖假设，并协调多个专门化智能体编排端到端发现流程，无需人工干预。框架使用多样化数据集与指标严格开展实验，通过自动化消融研究迭代改进方法，并借助闭环的模拟同行评审-反驳引擎验证研究发现。

Result: 以 ICLR、ICML、NeurIPS 等顶级会议录用的论文作为基准进行评估，ScientistTwo 自主生成专家级、可发表的论文与完整验证、可执行的代码库；其方案持续超越人类当前最优模型，并在自动化 AI 评审智能体下取得高于人类撰写论文的平均评审评分。

Conclusion: 结果表明 ScientistTwo 不仅是辅助工具，而是能够推动人类发现前沿的自主科学开拓者。

Abstract: Scientific discovery is defined by the ability to identify the boundaries of existing knowledge and venture into unexplored territory. The ultimate vision for AI in science is problem-driven autonomous research: given a fundamental challenge by a human expert, the AI independently navigates the scientific landscape, uncovers theoretical and empirical bottlenecks, and systematically expands the frontier of knowledge. In this paper, we introduce ScientistTwo, a fully autonomous multi-agent framework designed to realize this vision. Specifically, ScientistTwo takes an initial problem as input, establishes state-of-the-art baselines, formulates novel hypotheses, and coordinates specialized agents to orchestrate an end-to-end discovery cycle without human intervention. Moreover, the framework rigorously conducts experiments using diverse datasets and metrics, refines methodologies through automated ablation studies, and validates research findings via a closed-loop simulated peer-review rebuttal engine. To evaluate ScientistTwo's capabilities against the highest standards of human scientific achievement, we benchmark it across papers accepted at top-tier conferences such as ICLR, ICML, and NeurIPS. As a result, ScientistTwo autonomously generates expert-level, publishable papers and fully verified, executable codebases. Its solutions consistently outperform human state-of-the-art models, and achieve higher average review ratings than human-authored papers under automated AI review agents. These results show that ScientistTwo is not merely an assistive tool but an autonomous scientific pioneer capable of pushing the frontiers of human discovery. Project website: https://scientist-two.github.io/

</details>


### [243] [Replan, Repair, or Edit? A Unified Empirical Evaluation of Travel Agents for Itinerary Revision under Resource Disruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19654)
*Xiaofei Yuan,Yan Zhang,Shaobo Qiao,Huangleshuai He,Leyan Ni,Mingchen Ju,Lujia Yang,Sijia Xu,Yifu Tang,Zhengyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对旅行规划中的行程中断修复问题进行系统实证研究，比较LLM-Z3全量重规划、IPyHOPPER分层修复和iTIMO局部修订三类方法在有效性、稳定性和计算成本上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已接受的行程可能因航班取消、酒店不可用或景点关闭而变得不可行，而现有修复方法（全量重规划、经典计划修复、LLM旅行代理修订）任务设定与评估协议各异，难以直接比较。

Method: 基于两个TREK衍生基准：500个单一中断案例（含可行与不可行）和200个可行同时复合中断案例；对比LLM-Z3全量重规划、IPyHOPPER分层修复和iTIMO局部修订适配器，评估有效性、计划稳定性和计算成本。

Result: LLM-Z3配合Gemini在复合中断成功率最高；IPyHOPPER在单一中断总体成功率上接近该配置，且成功修复时保留了更多已接受行程内容。分层与局部修复的编辑更少、保留的已接受承诺更多。计算成本方面：IPyHOPPER无需LLM推理，LLM-Z3适配器为紧凑单次调用，iTIMO适配器消耗Token显著更多。

Conclusion: 研究为在所评估设定下平衡可行性恢复、承诺保持与计算成本提供了实用指南。

Abstract: Travel-planning agents generate itineraries that may become infeasible after acceptance because of flight cancellations, hotel unavailability, or attraction closures. Revising these itineraries involves full replanning, classical plan repair, and LLM-based travel-agent revision, whose differing task formulations and evaluation protocols hinder comparison. We conduct a systematic empirical study using two TREK-derived benchmark sets: 500 single-disruption cases, including feasible and infeasible instances, and 200 feasible simultaneous compound-disruption cases. We compare LLM-Z3 full replanning, IPyHOPPER hierarchical repair, and an iTIMO local-revision adapter across effectiveness, plan stability, and computational cost. LLM-Z3 with Gemini achieved the highest observed compound-disruption success. IPyHOPPER nearly matched that configuration's single-disruption overall success, while preserving substantially more of the accepted itinerary on successful repairs. Successful hierarchical and local repairs made fewer edits and retained more accepted commitments than full replanning. Computational profiles differed: IPyHOPPER used no LLM inference, the evaluated LLM-Z3 adapter used compact one-call inference, and the iTIMO adapter consumed substantially more tokens. The study provides practical guidelines for balancing feasibility recovery, commitment preservation, and computational cost within evaluated settings.

</details>


### [244] [When2Think: Learning Difficulty-Aware Length Control for Efficient Hybrid Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19671)
*Jaejun Shim,HyunJin Kim,Young Jin Kim,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出When2Think，一个针对大型推理模型的后训练框架，将高效推理建模为实例自适应计算分配问题，通过难度感知奖励塑造让模型在简单问题上直接作答、在困难问题上保持长推理，从而改善准确率与效率的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型存在系统性低效：在简单问题上过度思考，在困难问题上思考不足。现有基于统一长度惩罚或刚性路由的方法会产生"效率税"，即用简单问题上的计算节省换取困难问题上的准确率损失。作者希望解决这一准确率-效率之间的矛盾。

Method: 作者将高效推理形式化为实例自适应计算分配问题，并提出When2Think后训练框架。其核心是实例级难度感知控制（IDAC），一种奖励塑造机制，利用预先计算好的参考统计量（准确率与token用量）来调节推理深度。结合基于验证器的奖励与批次标准化优势，IDAC实现了无需评论家（critic）、无需学习奖励模型、无需在线参考模型查询的稳定优化。

Result: 在数学基准测试上，When2Think改善了准确率-效率权衡：在AIME24上，Pass@3相较基座模型提升10.0%，同时token用量减少27.9%；在AIME25上达到40.0% Pass@3，优于纯压缩方法和纯路由方法等基线。

Conclusion: When2Think通过难度感知的计算分配，使模型学会在何时使用系统1（NoThink）与系统2（Think），在保持困难问题推理能力的同时提升简单问题效率，验证了实例自适应计算分配在高效推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on complex tasks but exhibit systematic inefficiency: they often overthink easy problems and underthink hard ones. Existing approaches based on uniform length penalties or rigid routing incur an efficiency tax, trading reduced computation on easy instances for accuracy loss on hard instances. We formulate efficient reasoning as an instance-adaptive computation allocation problem and propose When2Think, a post-training framework for hybrid reasoning that dynamically allocates computation based on problem difficulty. Our method introduces Instance-level Difficulty-Aware Control (IDAC), a reward-shaping mechanism that leverages pre-computed reference statistics (accuracy and token usage) to regulate reasoning depth. Combined with verifier-based rewards and batch-wise standardized advantages, IDAC enables stable critic-free optimization without learned reward models or online reference-model queries. When2Think encourages direct answering on easy instances while preserving extended reasoning on hard instances, thereby learning when to use System 1 (NoThink) versus System 2 (Think). Experiments on mathematical benchmarks demonstrate improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs: on AIME24, Pass@3 increases by 10.0% while token usage is reduced by 27.9% relative to the base model, and on AIME25, When2Think achieves 40.0% Pass@3, outperforming compression and routing-only baselines.

</details>


### [245] [FINSKILLOPS: A Self-Evolving Multi-Agent System for SEC Filing QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19680)
*Yanzhang Ma,Zhenghan Tai,Hanwei Wu,Sizhe Guan,Jianliang Lei,Hailin He,Chaolong Jiang,Jijun Chi,Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Bohuai Xiao,Jingrui Tian,Xinlu Wu,Xingao Zhan,Peng Lu,Muzhi Li,Yihong Wu,Liheng Ma,Sicheng Lyu,Tianshuo Yan,Junhao Zhu,Yaqian Xu,Lei Ding,Yufei Cui,Ziquan Liu,Boyu Han,Hengli Liu,Ling Zhou,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出将金融问答系统的部署后改进视为"受控行为维护"，并实例化为FINSKILLOPS多智能体系统，通过技能补丁的定向验证、回归检查、负对照和版本化管理实现可靠自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融QA系统在部署前通过检索、提示或智能体协调进行优化，但部署后可靠性行为固定不变。实践中，新出现的SEC文件问题反复暴露期间、实体、证据使用和计算方面的异质错误。现有自我改进方法虽能将失败转化为新行为，但难以控制修正的适用范围，也无法避免破坏原本正确的答案。
